       DATE-COMPILED.   TODAY.  

      ******************************************************************
      * 
      *                         RSO28UGC
      *  Reservations subroutine (RSO) 28 Update Guest Cabin (UGC)  
      * 
      *  The purpose of this subroutine is to receive the complete data 
      *  for an existing IBR and all its active guests and then update  
      *  it on the database.
      * 
      *  This subroutine implements the database rules for cabin/guest  
      *  maintenance rather than business rules.  This means validation 
      *  or determination of data will not occur here.  This subroutine 
      *  is intended to be called by another which should have already  
      *  dealt with validation and determination of pricing and 
      *  other data before calling this.
      * 
      *  When update is successful, this subroutine will pass a picture 
      *  of the old data and the new data to RSO29GCH to update guest   
      *  cabin history. 
      * 
      *  Protocol for calling subroutine:   
      * 
      *      CALL 'RSO28UGC'      USING DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS
      *                                 DPP01-COMMON-INFO   
      *                                 RSP06-RSP06-OLD 
      *                                 RSP06-RSP06-NEW 
      *                                 RSP28-AUXILLARY-INPUT.  
      * 
      *      Load IBR and PAX data into RSP06 as it should look on  
      *      database.  Pax to be cancelled from IBR booking should be  
      *      loaded into the rsp06-cxp-pax-table. New PAx should not
      *      have a new SEQ-NUM assigned.  New  
      *      packages should have have blank PPR-PKG-NUM.   
      * 
************************************************************************

       ENVIRONMENT DIVISION.

       CONFIGURATION SECTION.   

       SOURCE-COMPUTER. UNISYS-2200.
       OBJECT-COMPUTER. UNISYS-2200 MEMORY SIZE 3 MODULES.  

       SPECIAL-NAMES.   
               PRINTER IS PRINTER   
               CONSOLE IS CONSOLE.  

       DATA DIVISION.   

       SUBSCHEMA SECTION.   

       INVOKE SUBSCHEMA PRD100UCOBSU
           IN FILE SUBSCHEMAS OF SCHEMA PRD100SCHEMA
           COPYING RECORDS         INTO COMMON  
           COPYING DATA-NAMES      INTO COMMON  
           OVERLAY PAX-REC         WITH PAX-CXF-REC 
169014*                                 PAX-FUT-REC 
                                        PAX-VI-REC  
           DMCA                         COMMON  
           ERROR RECOVERY            IS 9100-DATABASE-ERROR.

       WORKING-STORAGE SECTION.

       0020-INIT-CXP-PAX-RECS.  
           PERFORM VARYING PSUB FROM 1 BY 1 
                   UNTIL PSUB > RSP06-CXP-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-NEW  
              IF (RSP06-PCX-CANCEL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW (PSUB) NOT > ZERO) 
                 MOVE 6 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX 
                 GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG  
              END-IF
              IF (RSP06-PCX-STATUS OF RSP06-NEW (PSUB) <= SPACES)   
                 MOVE 'C' TO RSP06-PCX-STATUS OF RSP06-NEW (PSUB)   
              END-IF
              IF (RSP06-PCX-STATUS OF RSP06-NEW (PSUB) NOT = 'C')   
                 IF RSP29-REPRICE-FLAG = 1  
                    MOVE 'C' TO RSP06-PCX-STATUS OF RSP06-NEW (PSUB)
                 ELSE   
                    MOVE 7 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
                    GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG   
                 END-IF 
              END-IF

              IF RSP06-CTIR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (PSUB) = 'Y' 
                 MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW TO   
                      RSP06-CTIR-BOOK-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW (PSUB)  
              END-IF
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-OLD > SPACES  
              MOVE 'N' TO WS-NEW-BKG-FLAG   
           ELSE 
              MOVE 'Y'   TO WS-NEW-BKG-FLAG 
              MOVE 1     TO WS-NUMRTN-TYPE  
              MOVE SPACE TO WS-NUMRTN-COMMAND   
              CALL 'NUMRTN' USING WS-NUMRTN-BOOK
                                  WS-NUMRTN-TYPE
                                  WS-NUMRTN-COMMAND

              MOVE 8 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

       0040-VERIFY-NEXT-PAX.
           ADD 1 TO PSUB.   

           IF PSUB > RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-NEW 
              GO TO 0060-CHECK-GBR-UPDATE.  

           MOVE PSUB TO RSP06-PAX-CABIN-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (PSUB).
           IF RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW = 'G'
              MOVE GBR1-BOOK-NUM TO 
                   RSP06-PAX-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (PSUB)   
           ELSE 
              MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW TO   
                   RSP06-PAX-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (PSUB).  

           MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW TO  
                RSP06-PAX-CABIN-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (PSUB).   
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE OF RSP06-NEW TO 
                RSP06-PAX-SHIP-CODE OF RSP06-NEW (PSUB).
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW TO 
                RSP06-PAX-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW (PSUB).
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-DURATION-DAYS OF RSP06-NEW TO 
                RSP06-PAX-DURATION-DAYS OF RSP06-NEW (PSUB).
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-NEW TO 
                RSP06-PAX-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (PSUB).
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-CANCEL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW TO   
                RSP06-PAX-CANCEL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW (PSUB).  
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-CANCEL-TIME OF RSP06-NEW TO   
                RSP06-PAX-CANCEL-TIME OF RSP06-NEW (PSUB).  

           IF RSP06-IBR-CANCEL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW > ZERO 
              MOVE 'C' TO RSP06-PAX-STATUS OF RSP06-NEW (PSUB)  
           ELSE 
              IF (RSP06-PAX-STATUS OF RSP06-NEW (PSUB) = 'C')   
                 MOVE 9  TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
                 GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG. 

           IF RSP06-PPR-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (PSUB) > RSP06-PPR-TABLE-MAX   
              MOVE 10  TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

           IF RSP06-PIID-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (PSUB) > RSP06-PIID-TABLE-MAX 
              MOVE 11  TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

           IF RSP06-PICF-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (PSUB) > RSP06-PICF-TABLE-MAX

              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

CCLAUS              MOVE '5'  TO INDV-BOOKING-CALCAREA (9:1)

CCLAUS              MOVE '6'  TO INDV-BOOKING-CALCAREA (9:1)

CCLAUS              MOVE '7'  TO INDV-BOOKING-CALCAREA (9:1)

CCLAUS           WHEN 'N' WHEN 'P' WHEN '5' 
CCLAUS                 MOVE '5'  TO INDV-BOOKING-CALCAREA (9:1) 
CCLAUS           WHEN 'Q' WHEN 'R' WHEN '6' 
CCLAUS                 MOVE '6'  TO INDV-BOOKING-CALCAREA (9:1) 
CCLAUS           WHEN 'S' WHEN 'T' WHEN '7' 
CCLAUS                 MOVE '7'  TO INDV-BOOKING-CALCAREA (9:1) 
CCLAUS           WHEN 'V' WHEN 'W' WHEN '8' 
CCLAUS                 MOVE '8'  TO INDV-BOOKING-CALCAREA (9:1) 
CCLAUS           WHEN 'X' WHEN 'Z' WHEN '9' 
CCLAUS                 MOVE '9'  TO INDV-BOOKING-CALCAREA (9:1) 
CCLAUS        END-EVALUATE  
PPCPDT        PERFORM 42200-INSERT-CABIN-OFFER  
PPCPDT                THRU 42200-INSERT-CABIN-OFFER-EXIT
              STORE INDV-BOOKING-REC
                    ON ERROR GO TO 10100-STORE-IBR-ERROR
PPCPDT*       PERFORM 42200-INSERT-CABIN-OFFER  
PPCPDT*               THRU 42200-INSERT-CABIN-OFFER-EXIT
              MOVE 'Y' TO WS-NEW-SIBR-FLAG  
              PERFORM 8200-UPDATE-CABIN-BOOKING THRU 8299-EXIT  
              PERFORM ARP06-STORE-PMT-DUE-RECORDS THRU ARP06-EXIT   
              GO TO 0290-UPDATE-SIBR
           ELSE 

PPCPDT*       MODIFY INDV-BOOKING-REC RECORD
PPCPDT*              ON ERROR GO TO 10200-MODIFY-IBR-ERROR  
PPC470*              ON ERROR GO TO 10200-MODIFY-IBR-ERROR. 
PPC470        IF RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE OF RSP06-OLD NOT = 
PPC470           RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE OF RSP06-NEW   
PPC470           EXEC SQL   
PPC470              DELETE FROM CABIN_OFFER 
PPC470               WHERE SHIP_CODE = :RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE OF RSP06-OLD
PPC470                 AND BOOK_NUM = :RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-OLD  
PPC470           END-EXEC   
PPC470           IF SQLCODE < 0 
PPC470              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR   
PPC470           END-IF 
PPC470           PERFORM 42200-INSERT-CABIN-OFFER   
PPC470              THRU 42200-INSERT-CABIN-OFFER-EXIT  
PPC470        ELSE  
PPC470           PERFORM 42300-UPDATE-CABIN-OFFER   
PPC470              THRU 42300-UPDATE-CABIN-OFFER-EXIT  
PPC470        END-IF
PPCPDT        PERFORM 8200-UPDATE-CABIN-BOOKING THRU 8299-EXIT  
PPCPDT        MOVE 'Y' TO WS-MODIFY-IBR-DMS 
PPC470     END-IF.  
PPCPDT*    PERFORM 8200-UPDATE-CABIN-BOOKING THRU 8299-EXIT.

           PERFORM ARP06-MODIFY-PMT-DUE-BOOKING THRU ARP06-EXIT.

           IF WS-SAIL-CHG-FLAG = 'N'
              MOVE 'N' TO WS-NEW-SIBR-FLAG  
              GO TO 0290-UPDATE-SIBR.   

           MOVE 'Y'               TO WS-NEW-SIBR-FLAG.  
PPC470*    MOVE WS-OLD-SAIL-DBKEY TO GENERIC-DBK.   
PPC470* 
PPC470*    FETCH SAIL1-REC RECORD GENERIC-DBK   
PPC470*          ON ERROR GO TO 10300-OLD-SAIL-ERROR.   
PPC470     MOVE WS-OLD-SAIL1-SAIL-DATE    TO TDP01-I-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD 
PPC470     MOVE WS-OLD-SAIL1-SHIP-CODE    TO TDP01-I-SHIP-CODE  
PPC470     MOVE WS-OLD-SAIL1-DURATION-DAYS  
PPC470                                    TO TDP01-I-DUR-DAYS   
PPC470     PERFORM TDP01-RET-SAIL-DETAILS THRU  
PPC470                            TDP01-RET-SAIL-DETAILS-EXIT.  
PPC470     IF TDP01-SAIL-SQLERR 
PPC470         OR TDP01-SAIL-NOTFND 
PPC470            OR TDP01-PROC-ERROR > ZEROES  
PPC470            MOVE 503 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
PPC470            GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
PPC470     END-IF.  

           PERFORM DPP16-DELETE-SAIL-INDIVIDUAL THRU DPP16-EXIT.

       0290-UPDATE-SIBR.
PPC470*    IF (RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE OF RSP06-NEW NOT =   
PPC470*          SAIL1-SHIP-CODE) OR
PPC470*          (RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW NOT =
PPC470*          SAIL1-SAIL-DATE) OR

                                       TO TDP01-I-DUR-DAYS

PPC470                                  TDP01-RET-SAIL-DETAILS-EXIT 
PPC470           IF TDP01-SAIL-SQLERR   
PPC470               OR TDP01-SAIL-NOTFND   
PPC470                OR TDP01-PROC-ERROR > ZEROES  
PPC470                  MOVE 504 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
PPC470                  GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR   
PPC470           END-IF 
PPC470     END-IF.  

            IF WS-NEW-SIBR-FLAG = 'Y'   
               PERFORM DPP16-CREATE-SAIL-INDIVIDUAL THRU DPP16-EXIT

               PERFORM DPP16-MODIFY-SAIL-INDIVIDUAL THRU DPP16-EXIT.

       0560-UPDATE-TRAVEL-WITH. 
           IF WS-NEW-BKG-FLAG = 'Y' 
              GO TO 0580-CHECK-NEW-TWR. 

           IF RSP06-IBR-TRVL-WITH-NUM OF RSP06-OLD <= ZERO  
              GO TO 0580-CHECK-NEW-TWR. 

           IF RSP06-IBR-TRVL-WITH-NUM OF RSP06-NEW =
                 RSP06-IBR-TRVL-WITH-NUM OF RSP06-OLD   
              IF RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE OF RSP06-OLD = 
                    RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE OF RSP06-NEW AND
                    RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-OLD =  
                    RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW AND
                    RSP06-IBR-DURATION-DAYS OF RSP06-OLD =  
                    RSP06-IBR-DURATION-DAYS OF RSP06-NEW
                 IF RSP06-IBR-CANCEL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW =
                        RSP06-IBR-CANCEL-DATE OF RSP06-OLD  
                    GO TO 0600-PROCESS-WAITLIST.

           MOVE RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE OF RSP06-OLD TO TWR-SHIP-CODE.  
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-OLD TO TWR-SAIL-DATE.  
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-DURATION-DAYS OF RSP06-OLD TO 
                TWR-DURATION-DAYS.  
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-TRVL-WITH-NUM OF RSP06-OLD TO 
                TWR-TRVL-WITH-NUM.  

           FETCH TRAVEL-WITH-REC RECORD 
                 ON ERROR GO TO 25800-FETCH-TWR-ERROR.  

           MOVE ZERO TO TWR-SUB.

           PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL SUB > TWR-ENTRY-CTR
              IF TWR-BOOK-NUM (SUB) = RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-OLD   
                 MOVE SUB    TO TWR-SUB 
                 MOVE TWR-ENTRY-CTR TO SUB  
              END-IF
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF RSP06-IBR-CANCEL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW > ZERO 
              IF TWR-SUB > ZERO 
                 MOVE 'C' TO TWR-BOOK-STATUS (TWR-SUB)  
              ELSE  
                 GO TO 0600-PROCESS-WAITLIST
           ELSE 
              IF RSP06-IBR-TRVL-WITH-NUM OF RSP06-OLD = 
                    RSP06-IBR-TRVL-WITH-NUM OF RSP06-NEW
                 IF (RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE OF RSP06-NEW NOT = 
                       RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE OF RSP06-OLD) OR 
                       (RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW NOT =  
                       RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-OLD) OR 
                       (RSP06-IBR-DURATION-DAYS OF RSP06-NEW NOT =  
                       RSP06-IBR-DURATION-DAYS OF RSP06-OLD)
                    MOVE 30 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX 
                    GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG   
                 ELSE   
                    IF TWR-SUB = ZERO   
                      ADD 1              TO TWR-ENTRY-CTR   
                      MOVE TWR-ENTRY-CTR TO TWR-SUB 
                      MOVE SPACES        TO TWR-BOOK-ENTRY (TWR-SUB)
                      MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW TO   
                           TWR-BOOK-NUM (TWR-SUB)   
                    ELSE
                       IF TWR-BOOK-STATUS (TWR-SUB) = 'C' OR 'S'
                          MOVE SPACE TO TWR-BOOK-STATUS (TWR-SUB)   
                       ELSE 
                          GO TO 0600-PROCESS-WAITLIST   
              ELSE  
                 IF TWR-SUB > ZERO  
140294*             IF TWR-BOOK-STATUS (TWR-SUB) = 'C' OR 'S'   
140294              IF TWR-BOOK-STATUS (TWR-SUB) = 'S'  
                       NEXT SENTENCE
                    ELSE
135725                 MOVE 'S' TO TWR-BOOK-STATUS (TWR-SUB).   

           MOVE 0 TO POS-SUB.   

           PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
                   UNTIL SUB > TWR-ENTRY-CTR
              IF (TWR-BOOK-STATUS (SUB) NOT = 'S')  
                 ADD 1 TO POS-SUB   
                 MOVE TWR-BOOK-ENTRY (SUB) TO TWR-BOOK-ENTRY (POS-SUB)  
              END-IF
           END-PERFORM. 

           MOVE POS-SUB      TO N18-SIGNED. 
           MOVE N18-UNSIGNED TO TWR-ENTRY-CTR.  

           MODIFY TRAVEL-WITH-REC RECORD
                 ON ERROR GO TO 25900-MODIFY-TWR-ERROR. 

           IF RSP06-IBR-TRVL-WITH-NUM OF RSP06-OLD =
                 RSP06-IBR-TRVL-WITH-NUM OF RSP06-NEW   
              GO TO 0600-PROCESS-WAITLIST.  

       0580-CHECK-NEW-TWR.  
           IF RSP06-IBR-TRVL-WITH-NUM OF RSP06-NEW <= ZERO  
              GO TO 0600-PROCESS-WAITLIST.  

           IF RSP06-IBR-CANCEL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW > ZERO 
              MOVE 31 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX   
              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

           IF RSP28-NEW-TRAVEL-WITH-FLAG = 1
              MOVE LOW-VALUES TO TRAVEL-WITH-REC.   

           MOVE RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE OF RSP06-NEW TO TWR-SHIP-CODE.  
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW TO TWR-SAIL-DATE.  
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-DURATION-DAYS OF RSP06-NEW TO 
                TWR-DURATION-DAYS.  
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-TRVL-WITH-NUM OF RSP06-NEW TO 
                TWR-TRVL-WITH-NUM.  

           IF RSP28-NEW-TRAVEL-WITH-FLAG = ZERO 
              FETCH TRAVEL-WITH-REC RECORD  
                    ON ERROR GO TO 26000-FETCH-TWR-ERROR.   

           MOVE ZERO TO TWR-SUB.

           PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL SUB > TWR-ENTRY-CTR
              IF TWR-BOOK-NUM (SUB) = RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW   
                 MOVE SUB    TO TWR-SUB 
                 MOVE TWR-ENTRY-CTR TO SUB  
              END-IF
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF TWR-SUB = ZERO
              ADD 1              TO TWR-ENTRY-CTR   
              MOVE TWR-ENTRY-CTR TO TWR-SUB 
              MOVE SPACES TO TWR-BOOK-ENTRY (TWR-SUB)   
              MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW TO   
                   TWR-BOOK-NUM (TWR-SUB)   
           ELSE 
              MOVE SPACE TO TWR-BOOK-STATUS (TWR-SUB).  

           MOVE 0 TO POS-SUB.   

           PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
                   UNTIL SUB > TWR-ENTRY-CTR
              IF (TWR-BOOK-STATUS (SUB) NOT = 'C' AND 'S')  
                 ADD 1 TO POS-SUB   
                 MOVE TWR-BOOK-ENTRY (SUB) TO TWR-BOOK-ENTRY (POS-SUB)  
              END-IF
           END-PERFORM. 

           MOVE POS-SUB      TO N18-SIGNED. 
           MOVE N18-UNSIGNED TO TWR-ENTRY-CTR.  

           IF RSP28-NEW-TRAVEL-WITH-FLAG = ZERO 
              MODIFY TRAVEL-WITH-REC RECORD 
                    ON ERROR GO TO 26100-MODIFY-TWR-ERROR   
           ELSE 
              STORE TRAVEL-WITH-REC 
                    ON ERROR GO TO 26200-STORE-TWR-ERROR.

                RSP06-WAIT-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW.

                DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE.

       1080-PROCESS-PAX.
           IF WS-NEW-PAX-FLAG = 'N' 
              IF OSUB = ZERO AND OCSUB = ZERO   
                 MOVE 43 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
                 GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG  
              ELSE  
                 IF OSUB > ZERO AND OCSUB > ZERO
                    MOVE 44 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX 
                    GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.  

           IF NSUB = ZERO AND NCSUB = ZERO  
              MOVE 45 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX   
              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

           IF NSUB > ZERO AND NCSUB > ZERO  
              MOVE 46 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX   
              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

           IF WS-NEW-PAX-FLAG = 'Y' 
              IF NSUB = ZERO OR OSUB > ZERO OR OCSUB > ZERO 
                 MOVE 47 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
                 GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG  
              ELSE  
                 MOVE 'N' TO WS-PAX-UPDATE-CODE 
                 GO TO 1100-UPDATE-PAX-REC  
           ELSE 
              IF NSUB > ZERO
                 IF OSUB > ZERO 
                    MOVE 'U' TO WS-PAX-UPDATE-CODE  
                 ELSE   
                    MOVE 48 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX 
                    GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG   
              ELSE  
                 IF OSUB > ZERO 
                    MOVE 'C' TO WS-PAX-UPDATE-CODE  
                 ELSE   
161361*             M3E Note:  PAX isn't in either old or new   
161361*             RSP06 active tables.  It must be new in the cancel  
161361*             table.  
                    MOVE 'M' TO WS-PAX-UPDATE-CODE. 

           IF WS-PAX-UPDATE-CODE = 'C' OR 'U'   
              IF (PAX-UPDATE-DATETIME NOT = 
                    RSP06-PAX-UPDATE-DATETIME OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB)) OR   
                    (PAX-LAST-UPDATE-NUM NOT =  
                    RSP06-PAX-LAST-UPDATE-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB))  
                 MOVE 49 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
                 GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG  
              END-IF
           ELSE 
              IF (PAX-UPDATE-DATETIME NOT = 
                    RSP06-PCX-UPDATE-DATETIME OF RSP06-OLD (OCSUB)) OR  
                    (PAX-LAST-UPDATE-NUM NOT =  
                    RSP06-PCX-LAST-UPDATE-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (OCSUB)) 
                 MOVE 50 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
                 GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG. 

       1100-UPDATE-PAX-REC. 
           IF WS-PAX-UPDATE-CODE = 'N'  
              GO TO 1140-UPDATE-PAX-DATA.   

           IF WS-PAX-UPDATE-CODE = 'U'  
              IF RSP06-PAX-REC OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) =
                    RSP06-PAX-REC OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB)   
                 PERFORM 8000-ADD-TO-PROCESS-TABLE THRU 8009-EXIT   
                 GO TO 1240-CHECK-PTFR-ENTRY
              END-IF
           ELSE 
              IF WS-PAX-UPDATE-CODE = 'M'   
                 IF RSP06-CXP-PAX-REC OF RSP06-NEW (NCSUB) =
                       RSP06-CXP-PAX-REC OF RSP06-OLD (OCSUB)   
                    GO TO 1240-CHECK-PTFR-ENTRY 
                 ELSE   
                    GO TO 1120-PROCESS-PAX-UPDATE.  

PPC470*    IF RSP06-PAX-DEV-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) = ZERO                 *
PPC470*       GO TO 1120-PROCESS-PAX-UPDATE.

PPC470*    IF (RSP06-PAX-FARE-TYPE OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) = 'C')   
PPC470*       GO TO 1120-PROCESS-PAX-UPDATE.

PPC470*    IF WS-PAX-UPDATE-CODE = 'C'  
PPC470*       MOVE 'C' TO WS-DEV-MOD-CODE   
PPC470*       PERFORM 8050-LOAD-DEV-TABLE THRU 8059-EXIT
PPC470*       GO TO 1120-PROCESS-PAX-UPDATE.

PPC470*    IF RSP06-IBR-CANCEL-DATE OF RSP06-OLD > ZERO 
PPC470*       GO TO 1120-PROCESS-PAX-UPDATE.

PPC470*    IF (RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-OLD NOT =   
PPC470*          RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW) OR   
PPC470*          (RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE OF RSP06-OLD NOT =
PPC470*          RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE OF RSP06-NEW) OR   
PPC470*          (RSP06-IBR-DURATION-DAYS OF RSP06-OLD NOT =
PPC470*          RSP06-IBR-DURATION-DAYS OF RSP06-NEW)  
PPC470*       MOVE 'C' TO WS-DEV-MOD-CODE   
PPC470*       PERFORM 8050-LOAD-DEV-TABLE THRU 8059-EXIT
PPC470*       GO TO 1120-PROCESS-PAX-UPDATE.

PPC470*    IF RSP06-PAX-FARE-TYPE OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) NOT = 
PPC470*          RSP06-PAX-FARE-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB)
PPC470*       MOVE 'C' TO WS-DEV-MOD-CODE   
PPC470*       PERFORM 8050-LOAD-DEV-TABLE THRU 8059-EXIT
PPC470*       GO TO 1120-PROCESS-PAX-UPDATE.

PPC470*    IF RSP06-PPR-CNT OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) NOT =   
PPC470*          RSP06-PPR-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB)  
PPC470*       MOVE 'C' TO WS-DEV-MOD-CODE   
PPC470*       PERFORM 8050-LOAD-DEV-TABLE THRU 8059-EXIT
PPC470*       GO TO 1120-PROCESS-PAX-UPDATE.

PPC470*    IF RSP06-PPR-CNT OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) > ZERO  
PPC470*       MOVE ZERO TO PPR-SUB  
PPC470*       PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
PPC470*               UNTIL SUB > RSP06-PPR-CNT OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) 
PPC470*          IF (RSP06-PPR-PKG-TYPE OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB SUB) NOT =   
PPC470*              RSP06-PPR-PKG-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB SUB)) OR 
PPC470*             (RSP06-PPR-PKG-CODE OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB SUB) NOT =   
146816*              RSP06-PPR-PKG-CODE OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB SUB))
PPC470*              RSP06-PPR-PKG-CODE OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB SUB)) OR 
PPC470*             (RSP06-PPR-CANCEL-DATE OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB SUB) NOT =
PPC470*              RSP06-PPR-CANCEL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB SUB)) 
PPC470*             MOVE SUB TO PPR-SUB 
PPC470*          END-IF 
PPC470*       END-PERFORM   
PPC470*       IF PPR-SUB > ZERO 
PPC470*          MOVE 'C' TO WS-DEV-MOD-CODE
PPC470*          PERFORM 8050-LOAD-DEV-TABLE THRU 8059-EXIT 
PPC470*          GO TO 1120-PROCESS-PAX-UPDATE. 

PPC470*    IF RSP06-PAX-CITY-CODE OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) NOT = 
PPC470*          RSP06-PAX-CITY-CODE OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB)
PPC470*       MOVE 'M' TO WS-DEV-MOD-CODE   
PPC470*       PERFORM 8050-LOAD-DEV-TABLE THRU 8059-EXIT
PPC470*       GO TO 1120-PROCESS-PAX-UPDATE.

PPC470*    IF (RSP06-PAX-FIRST-NAME OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) NOT =   
PPC470*          RSP06-PAX-FIRST-NAME OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB)) OR   
PPC470*          (RSP06-PAX-LAST-NAME OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) NOT = 
PPC470*          RSP06-PAX-LAST-NAME OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB)) OR
PPC470*          (RSP06-PAX-TITLE OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) NOT = 
PPC470*          RSP06-PAX-TITLE OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB))   
PPC470*       MOVE 'M' TO WS-DEV-MOD-CODE   
PPC470*       PERFORM 8050-LOAD-DEV-TABLE THRU 8059-EXIT
PPC470*       GO TO 1120-PROCESS-PAX-UPDATE.

       1120-PROCESS-PAX-UPDATE. 
           MOVE PAX-SHIP-CODE                    TO WS-OLD-SHIP-CODE.   
           MOVE PAX-SAIL-DATE                    TO WS-OLD-SAIL-DATE.   
           MOVE PAX-CABIN-NUM                    TO WS-OLD-CABIN-NUM.   
           MOVE PAX-CABIN-SEQ-NUM                TO 
                WS-OLD-CABIN-SEQ-NUM.   
           MOVE PAX-DURATION-DAYS                TO 
                WS-OLD-DURATION-DAYS.   
           MOVE PAX-LAST-NAME                    TO WS-OLD-LAST-NAME.   
           MOVE PAX-FIRST-NAME                   TO WS-OLD-FIRST-NAME.  
           MOVE PAX-TITLE                        TO WS-OLD-TITLE.   
           MOVE PAX-PAST-GUEST-NUM               TO 
                WS-OLD-PAST-GUEST-NUM.  

           MOVE 'N' TO WS-PAX-SAIL-CHG-FLAG 
                       WS-PAX-CABIN-CHG-FLAG.   

           IF (RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE OF RSP06-NEW NOT = PAX-SHIP-CODE) OR 
                 (RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW NOT = PAX-SAIL-DATE) 
                 OR (RSP06-IBR-DURATION-DAYS OF RSP06-NEW NOT = 
                 PAX-DURATION-DAYS) 
              MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PAX-SAIL-CHG-FLAG. 

           IF (RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-NEW NOT = PAX-CABIN-NUM)
              MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PAX-CABIN-CHG-FLAG.

       1140-UPDATE-PAX-DATA.
           IF WS-PAX-UPDATE-CODE = 'N' OR 'U'   
              MOVE DPP02-SYSTEM-DATETIME TO 
                   RSP06-PAX-UPDATE-DATETIME OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB)
              MOVE DPP02-SYSTEM-DATE TO PAX-CHANGE-DATE 
                   RSP06-PAX-CHANGE-DATE OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB)
              IF RSP06-PAX-LAST-UPDATE-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) = 9  
                 MOVE ZERO TO   
                      RSP06-PAX-LAST-UPDATE-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) 
              ELSE  
                 ADD 1 TO   
                     RSP06-PAX-LAST-UPDATE-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB)  
              END-IF
              MOVE RSP06-PAX-REC OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) TO PAX-REC 
           ELSE 
              MOVE DPP02-SYSTEM-DATETIME TO 
                   RSP06-PCX-UPDATE-DATETIME OF RSP06-NEW (NCSUB)   
              MOVE DPP02-SYSTEM-DATE TO PAX-CHANGE-DATE 
                   RSP06-PCX-CHANGE-DATE OF RSP06-NEW (NCSUB)   
              IF RSP06-PCX-LAST-UPDATE-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (NCSUB) = 9 
                 MOVE ZERO TO   
                      RSP06-PCX-LAST-UPDATE-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (NCSUB)
              ELSE  
                 ADD 1 TO   
                     RSP06-PCX-LAST-UPDATE-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (NCSUB) 
              END-IF
              MOVE RSP06-CXP-PAX-REC OF RSP06-NEW (NCSUB) TO PAX-REC.   

           IF WS-PAX-UPDATE-CODE = 'N'  
              GO TO 1160-UPDATE-PAX-DB. 

169014*    IF SAIL1-STATUS-CODE = 'F'   
169014*       IF (PAX-RECORD NOT = 'PAX-FUT-REC')   
169014*          MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PAX-REC-TYPE-CHG-FLAG   
169014*       END-IF
169014*    ELSE 
              IF PAX-CANCEL-DATE > ZERO 
                 IF (PAX-RECORD NOT = 'PAX-CXF-REC')
                     MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PAX-REC-TYPE-CHG-FLAG   
                 END-IF 
              ELSE  
                 IF PAX-CABIN-NUM (1:1) = 'T' OR 'C' OR 'W' OR 'Q'  
                    IF (PAX-RECORD NOT = 'PAX-VI-REC')  
                       MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PAX-REC-TYPE-CHG-FLAG 
                    END-IF  
                 ELSE   
                    IF (PAX-RECORD NOT = 'PAX-REC') 
                       MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PAX-REC-TYPE-CHG-FLAG.

           IF WS-PAX-SAIL-CHG-FLAG = 'N'
              IF WS-PAX-CABIN-CHG-FLAG = 'N'
                 IF WS-PAX-REC-TYPE-CHG-FLAG = 'N'  
                    MODIFY PAX-RECORD RECORD
                           ON ERROR GO TO 14000-PAX-MODIFY-ERROR
                    PERFORM 8000-ADD-TO-PROCESS-TABLE THRU 8009-EXIT
119799              MOVE 'M'  TO WS-UPDATE-FLAG 
119799              PERFORM 8300-UPDATE-PNR-REC THRU 8399-EXIT  
169014*             IF PAX-BOOK-TYPE = 'G'  
169014*                MOVE 'M' TO WS-UPDATE-FLAG   
169014*                PERFORM 8200-UPDATE-GCIR-REC THRU 8299-EXIT  
169014*             END-IF  
                    GO TO 1240-CHECK-PTFR-ENTRY.

           MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PAX-REC-TYPE-CHG-FLAG.

           DELETE PAX-RECORD RECORD 
                  ON ERROR GO TO 14100-PAX-DELETE-ERROR.

       1160-UPDATE-PAX-DB.  
           MOVE WS-NEW-PAX-HDR-DBKEY TO GENERIC-DBK.

           FIND PAX-HDR-REC RECORD GENERIC-DBK  
                ON ERROR GO TO 14200-FIND-PAX-HDR-ERROR.

169014*    IF SAIL1-STATUS-CODE = 'F'   
169014*       MOVE 'PAX-FUT-REC' TO PAX-RECORD  
169014*    ELSE 
              IF PAX-CANCEL-DATE > ZERO 
                 MOVE 'PAX-CXF-REC' TO PAX-RECORD   
CCLAUS*          MOVE PAX-BOOK-NUM (6:1) TO PAX-CXF-CALCAREA (8:1)  
CCLAUS           EVALUATE PAX-BOOK-NUM (6:1)
CCLAUS              WHEN 'B' WHEN 'C' WHEN '0'  
CCLAUS                    MOVE '0'  TO PAX-CXF-CALCAREA (8:1)   
CCLAUS              WHEN 'D' WHEN 'F' WHEN '1'  
CCLAUS                    MOVE '1'  TO PAX-CXF-CALCAREA (8:1)   
CCLAUS              WHEN 'G' WHEN 'H' WHEN '2'  
CCLAUS                    MOVE '2'  TO PAX-CXF-CALCAREA (8:1)   
CCLAUS              WHEN 'J' WHEN 'K' WHEN '3'  
CCLAUS                    MOVE '3'  TO PAX-CXF-CALCAREA (8:1)   
CCLAUS              WHEN 'L' WHEN 'M' WHEN '4'  
CCLAUS                    MOVE '4'  TO PAX-CXF-CALCAREA (8:1)   
CCLAUS              WHEN 'N' WHEN 'P' WHEN '5'  
CCLAUS                    MOVE '5'  TO PAX-CXF-CALCAREA (8:1)   
CCLAUS              WHEN 'Q' WHEN 'R' WHEN '6'  
CCLAUS                    MOVE '6'  TO PAX-CXF-CALCAREA (8:1)   
CCLAUS              WHEN 'S' WHEN 'T' WHEN '7'  
CCLAUS                    MOVE '7'  TO PAX-CXF-CALCAREA (8:1)   
CCLAUS              WHEN 'V' WHEN 'W' WHEN '8'  
CCLAUS                    MOVE '8'  TO PAX-CXF-CALCAREA (8:1)   
CCLAUS              WHEN 'X' WHEN 'Z' WHEN '9'  
CCLAUS                    MOVE '9'  TO PAX-CXF-CALCAREA (8:1)   
CCLAUS           END-EVALUATE   
              ELSE  
                 IF PAX-CABIN-NUM (1:1) = 'T' OR 'C' OR 'W' OR 'Q'  
                    MOVE 'PAX-VI-REC' TO PAX-RECORD 
                 ELSE   
CCLAUS*          MOVE PAX-BOOK-NUM (6:1) TO PAX-CXF-CALCAREA (8:1)  
CCLAUS              EVALUATE PAX-BOOK-NUM (6:1) 
CCLAUS                 WHEN 'B' WHEN 'C' WHEN '0'   
CCLAUS                       MOVE '0'  TO PAX-CXF-CALCAREA (8:1)
CCLAUS                 WHEN 'D' WHEN 'F' WHEN '1'   
CCLAUS                       MOVE '1'  TO PAX-CXF-CALCAREA (8:1)
CCLAUS                 WHEN 'G' WHEN 'H' WHEN '2'   
CCLAUS                       MOVE '2'  TO PAX-CXF-CALCAREA (8:1)
CCLAUS                 WHEN 'J' WHEN 'K' WHEN '3'   
CCLAUS                       MOVE '3'  TO PAX-CXF-CALCAREA (8:1)
CCLAUS                 WHEN 'L' WHEN 'M' WHEN '4'   
CCLAUS                       MOVE '4'  TO PAX-CXF-CALCAREA (8:1)
CCLAUS                 WHEN 'N' WHEN 'P' WHEN '5'   
CCLAUS                       MOVE '5'  TO PAX-CXF-CALCAREA (8:1)
CCLAUS                 WHEN 'Q' WHEN 'R' WHEN '6'   
CCLAUS                       MOVE '6'  TO PAX-CXF-CALCAREA (8:1)
CCLAUS                 WHEN 'S' WHEN 'T' WHEN '7'   
CCLAUS                       MOVE '7'  TO PAX-CXF-CALCAREA (8:1)
CCLAUS                 WHEN 'V' WHEN 'W' WHEN '8'   
CCLAUS                       MOVE '8'  TO PAX-CXF-CALCAREA (8:1)
CCLAUS                 WHEN 'X' WHEN 'Z' WHEN '9'   
CCLAUS                       MOVE '9'  TO PAX-CXF-CALCAREA (8:1)
CCLAUS              END-EVALUATE
                    MOVE 'PAX-REC' TO PAX-RECORD.   

           STORE PAX-RECORD 
                 ON ERROR GO TO 14300-STORE-PAX-ERROR.  

           MOVE CURRENT-DBKEY TO WS-PAX-DBKEY.  

           IF WS-PAX-UPDATE-CODE = 'N' OR 'U'   
              MOVE CURRENT-DBKEY TO RSP06-PAX-DBK OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB)   
           ELSE 
              MOVE CURRENT-DBKEY TO 
                   RSP06-CXP-PAX-DBK OF RSP06-NEW (NCSUB).  

           INSERT PAX-RECORD RECORD INTO INDV-BOOKING-PAX-SET   
                  ON ERROR GO TO 14400-INSERT-PAX-ERROR.

           IF WS-NEW-BKG-FLAG = 'Y' 
              PERFORM 8000-ADD-TO-PROCESS-TABLE THRU 8009-EXIT  
           ELSE 
              PERFORM 8010-ADD-TO-MOVE-TABLE THRU 8019-EXIT.

169014*    IF (PAX-BOOK-TYPE = 'G') 
169014*       IF WS-PAX-UPDATE-CODE = 'N'   
169014*          MOVE 'S' TO WS-UPDATE-FLAG 
169014*       ELSE  
169014*          MOVE 'M' TO WS-UPDATE-FLAG 
169014*       END-IF
169014*       PERFORM 8200-UPDATE-GCIR-REC THRU 8299-EXIT.  

           IF WS-PAX-UPDATE-CODE = 'N'  
              MOVE 'S' TO WS-UPDATE-FLAG
           ELSE 
              MOVE 'M' TO WS-UPDATE-FLAG.   

           PERFORM 8300-UPDATE-PNR-REC THRU 8399-EXIT.  

           IF WS-PAX-UPDATE-CODE = 'N'  
              MOVE 'S' TO WS-UPDATE-FLAG
           ELSE 
              MOVE 'M' TO WS-UPDATE-FLAG.   

           PERFORM 8400-UPDATE-PSR-REC THRU 8499-EXIT.  

           IF WS-PAX-UPDATE-CODE = 'N'  
              MOVE 'S' TO WS-UPDATE-FLAG
           ELSE 
              MOVE 'M' TO WS-UPDATE-FLAG.   

           PERFORM 8500-UPDATE-CBPR-REC THRU 8599-EXIT.

146816                             TO PAX-TRANSFER-REC

146816              PTFR-BOOK-NUM) OR   
146816             (RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) NOT = 
146816              PTFR-PAX-SEQ-NUM)   
146816              MOVE 108 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
146816              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG   
146816         END-IF   
146816     ELSE 
146816         MOVE RSP06-CPTFR-INFO OF RSP06-NEW (NCSUB)   
146816                             TO PAX-TRANSFER-REC  
146816         IF  (RSP06-PCX-CABIN-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (NCSUB) NOT = 
146816              PTFR-BOOK-NUM) OR   
146816             (RSP06-PCX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (NCSUB) NOT =
146816              PTFR-PAX-SEQ-NUM)   
146816              MOVE 108 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
146816              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG   
146816         END-IF   
146816     END-IF.  

           IF WS-UPDATE-FLAG = 'S'  
              STORE PAX-TRANSFER-REC
                    ON ERROR GO TO 14700-STORE-PTFR-ERROR   
           ELSE 
              MODIFY PAX-TRANSFER-REC RECORD
                    ON ERROR GO TO 14800-MODIFY-PTFR-ERROR.

PAXOBC*1252-GET-OLD-POBC.   
PAXOBC*    MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-OLD  TO POBC-BOOK-NUM.

PAXOBC*

PAXOBC*          ON ERROR GO TO 34100-FETCH-POBC-ERROR. 
PAXOBC* 
PAXOBC*    MOVE FUNCTION LENGTH (PAX-OBC-REC) TO REC-LEN.   
161361  
161361*    Fetching POBC using key fields from RSP06-IBR and RSP06-PAX  
161361*    rather than from RSP06-POBC as well as the following check   
161361*    will ensure that the right POBC in RSP06 is matched up with  
161361*    right PAX.   
PAXOBC*    IF RSP06-POBC-INFO OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) (1:REC-LEN) NOT = 
PAXOBC*          PAX-OBC-REC (1:REC-LEN)
PAXOBC*       MOVE 261 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
PAXOBC*       GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.
161361  
161361 1254-UPDATE-POBC-REC.
161361     IF WS-UPDATE-FLAG = 'D'  
161361*       This will also delete any POAR in automatic set.  
PAXOBC*       DELETE PAX-OBC-REC RECORD 
PAXOBC*              ON ERROR GO TO 34200-DELETE-POBC-ERROR 
PAXOBC        PERFORM VARYING POBC FROM 1 BY 1  
PAXOBC                  UNTIL POBC > RSP06-POBC-ENTRY-CNT   
PAXOBC                               OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB)
PAXOBC           PERFORM 60000-DELETE-GUEST-OBC 
PAXOBC              THRU 60000-DGO-EXIT 
PAXOBC        END-PERFORM   
161361        GO TO 1300-CHECK-PXFR-INFO.   
161361  
PAXOBC*    MOVE RSP06-POBC-INFO OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) TO PAX-OBC-REC. 
161361  
161361*    The following will check that the RSP06-POBC keys
161361*    correctly matched IBR and PAX.   
PAXOBC*    IF (RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW NOT = POBC-BOOK-NUM) OR  
PAXOBC*          (RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) NOT =   
PAXOBC*          POBC-PAX-SEQ-NUM)  

PAXOBC     IF (RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM     OF RSP06-NEW NOT =
PAXOBC         RSP06-POBC-BOOK-NUM    OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB)) OR   
PAXOBC        (RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM      OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) NOT = 
PAXOBC         RSP06-POBC-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB))  
161361        MOVE 262 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
161361        GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.
161361  
PAXOBC*    IF WS-UPDATE-FLAG = 'S'  
CCLAUS*       MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (6:1)
CCLAUS*                         TO PAXOBC-CALCAREA (7:1)
PAXOBC*       EVALUATE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (6:1)
PAXOBC*          WHEN 'B' WHEN 'C' WHEN '0' 
PAXOBC*                MOVE '0'  TO PAXOBC-CALCAREA (7:1)   
PAXOBC*          WHEN 'D' WHEN 'F' WHEN '1' 
PAXOBC*                MOVE '1'  TO PAXOBC-CALCAREA (7:1)   
PAXOBC*          WHEN 'G' WHEN 'H' WHEN '2' 
PAXOBC*                MOVE '2'  TO PAXOBC-CALCAREA (7:1)   
PAXOBC*          WHEN 'J' WHEN 'K' WHEN '3' 
PAXOBC*                MOVE '3'  TO PAXOBC-CALCAREA (7:1)   
PAXOBC*          WHEN 'L' WHEN 'M' WHEN '4' 
PAXOBC*                MOVE '4'  TO PAXOBC-CALCAREA (7:1)   
PAXOBC*          WHEN 'N' WHEN 'P' WHEN '5' 
PAXOBC*                MOVE '5'  TO PAXOBC-CALCAREA (7:1)   
PAXOBC*          WHEN 'Q' WHEN 'R' WHEN '6' 
PAXOBC*                MOVE '6'  TO PAXOBC-CALCAREA (7:1)   
PAXOBC*          WHEN 'S' WHEN 'T' WHEN '7' 
PAXOBC*                MOVE '7'  TO PAXOBC-CALCAREA (7:1)   
PAXOBC*          WHEN 'V' WHEN 'W' WHEN '8' 
PAXOBC*                MOVE '8'  TO PAXOBC-CALCAREA (7:1)   
PAXOBC*          WHEN 'X' WHEN 'Z' WHEN '9' 
PAXOBC*                MOVE '9'  TO PAXOBC-CALCAREA (7:1)   
PAXOBC*       END-EVALUATE  
PAXOBC*       STORE PAX-OBC-REC 
PAXOBC* 
PAXOBC*             ON ERROR GO TO 34300-STORE-POBC-ERROR   
PAXOBC*    ELSE 
PAXOBC*       MODIFY PAX-OBC-REC RECORD 
PAXOBC*             ON ERROR GO TO 34400-MODIFY-POBC-ERROR. 

PAXOBC     IF WS-UPDATE-FLAG = 'S'

PAXOBC                  UNTIL POBC > RSP06-POBC-ENTRY-CNT   
PAXOBC                               OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB)
PAXOBC           PERFORM 61000-INSERT-GUEST-OBC 
PAXOBC              THRU 61000-IGO-EXIT 
PAXOBC        END-PERFORM   
PAXOBC     ELSE 
PAXOBC        PERFORM VARYING POBC FROM 1 BY 1  
PAXOBC                  UNTIL POBC > RSP06-POBC-ENTRY-CNT   
PAXOBC                               OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB)
PAXOBC           PERFORM 62000-UPDATE-GUEST-OBC 
PAXOBC              THRU 62000-UGO-EXIT 
PAXOBC        END-PERFORM   
PAXOBC* Delete additional entries if the new entries are less   
PAXOBC        IF RSP06-POBC-ENTRY-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) < 
PAXOBC           RSP06-POBC-ENTRY-CNT OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB)   
PAXOBC           ADD 1 TO RSP06-POBC-ENTRY-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB)  
PAXOBC                    GIVING NENT   
PAXOBC           MOVE RSP06-POBC-ENTRY-CNT OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB)  
PAXOBC             TO OENT  
PAXOBC           PERFORM VARYING POBC FROM NENT BY 1
PAXOBC                     UNTIL POBC > OENT
PAXOBC              PERFORM 60000-DELETE-GUEST-OBC  
PAXOBC                 THRU 60000-DGO-EXIT  
PAXOBC           END-PERFORM
PAXOBC     END-IF.

161361 1255-UPDATE-POBC-POAR.   
161361*    Now compare old and new POBC entries and write adjustment
161361*    records for each manual (Navigator source) OBC changed   
161361*    (by table position).  Also write adjustment for system OBC   
161361*    if charged to the booking.  Adjustments don't get written
161361*    for new entries. 
161361     IF OSUB <= ZEROS 
161361        GO TO 1300-CHECK-PXFR-INFO.   
179105  
179105     IF RSP06-POBC-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) = 'N'
179105        GO TO 1300-CHECK-PXFR-INFO.   
161361  
161361     PERFORM  
161361         VARYING  POBC-SUB
161361             FROM 1 BY 1  
161361         UNTIL   (  POBC-SUB  
161361                 >  RSP06-POBC-ENTRY-CNT OF RSP06-OLD 
161361                        (OSUB))   
161361  
161361       IF      (  POBC-SUB
161361               <= RSP06-POBC-ENTRY-CNT OF RSP06-NEW   
161361                      (NSUB)) 
161361           AND (  RSP06-POBC-CHARGE-AMT OF RSP06-NEW  
161361                      (NSUB, POBC-SUB)
161361               =  RSP06-POBC-CHARGE-AMT OF RSP06-OLD  
161361                      (OSUB, POBC-SUB)
161361               )  
161361           AND (  RSP06-POBC-SAILING-AMT OF RSP06-NEW 
161361                      (NSUB, POBC-SUB)
161361               =  RSP06-POBC-SAILING-AMT OF RSP06-OLD 
161361                      (OSUB, POBC-SUB)
161361               )  

161361           AND (  RSP06-POBC-PCT OF RSP06-NEW 
161361                      (NSUB, POBC-SUB)
161361               =  RSP06-POBC-PCT OF RSP06-OLD 
161361                      (OSUB, POBC-SUB)
161361               )  
161361           AND (  RSP06-POBC-DATE OF RSP06-NEW
161361                      (NSUB, POBC-SUB)
161361               =  RSP06-POBC-DATE OF RSP06-OLD
161361                      (OSUB, POBC-SUB)
161361               )  
161361           AND (  RSP06-POBC-TIME OF RSP06-NEW
161361                      (NSUB, POBC-SUB)
161361               =  RSP06-POBC-TIME OF RSP06-OLD
161361                      (OSUB, POBC-SUB)
161361               )

161361                      (NSUB, POBC-SUB)
161361               =  RSP06-POBC-OBC-CODE OF RSP06-OLD
161361                      (OSUB, POBC-SUB)
161361               )

161361                      (NSUB, POBC-SUB)
161361               =  RSP06-POBC-REASON OF RSP06-OLD  
161361                      (OSUB, POBC-SUB)
161361               )  
161361           AND (  RSP06-POBC-SRC-CODE OF RSP06-NEW
161361                      (NSUB, POBC-SUB)
161361               =  RSP06-POBC-SRC-CODE OF RSP06-OLD
161361                      (OSUB, POBC-SUB)
161361               )  
161361           AND (  RSP06-POBC-PAYMENT-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW
161361                      (NSUB, POBC-SUB)
161361               =  RSP06-POBC-PAYMENT-TYPE OF RSP06-OLD
161361                      (OSUB, POBC-SUB)
161361               )  
161361*        No change to this POBC entry.
161361         CONTINUE 
161361       ELSE   
161361*        POR entry has fields changed that can be memorialized
161361*        on POAR. 
161361  
161361         IF      (  RSP06-POBC-SRC-CODE OF RSP06-OLD  
161361                        (OSUB, POBC-SUB)  
161361                 =  'N')

179105             OR  (  RSP06-POBC-OBC-CODE OF RSP06-OLD

179105                 = 'GPAS')

168921*             GO TO 1360-CHECK-ANAR-INFO  
168921              GO TO 1420-CHECK-PIAR-INFO  
                 ELSE   
                    MOVE 'D' TO WS-UPDATE-FLAG  
                    GO TO 1320-GET-OLD-PXFR.

           IF WS-PAX-UPDATE-CODE = 'N'

168921           GO TO 1420-CHECK-PIAR-INFO 
              ELSE

                       MOVE 110 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX 
                       GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG

168921           END-IF 
168921        END-IF
168921     END-IF.  

           IF (RSP06-PXFR-READ-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) NOT =
                 RSP06-PXFR-READ-IND OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB))

       1320-GET-OLD-PXFR.   
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-OLD  TO PXFR-BOOK-NUM.  
           MOVE RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) TO
                PXFR-PAX-SEQ-NUM.   
           MOVE 'PAXFEE'     TO PAXFEE-CALCAREA.

CCLAUS*    EVALUATE IBR-BOOK-NUM (6:1)  
CCLAUS*       WHEN '0' MOVE '1'    TO PAXFEE-CALCAREA (7:1) 
CCLAUS*       WHEN '1' MOVE '1'    TO PAXFEE-CALCAREA (7:1) 
CCLAUS*       WHEN '2' MOVE '2'    TO PAXFEE-CALCAREA (7:1) 
CCLAUS*       WHEN '3' MOVE '2'    TO PAXFEE-CALCAREA (7:1) 
CCLAUS*       WHEN '4' MOVE '3'    TO PAXFEE-CALCAREA (7:1) 
CCLAUS*       WHEN '5' MOVE '3'    TO PAXFEE-CALCAREA (7:1) 
CCLAUS*       WHEN '6' MOVE '4'    TO PAXFEE-CALCAREA (7:1) 
CCLAUS*       WHEN '7' MOVE '4'    TO PAXFEE-CALCAREA (7:1) 
CCLAUS*       WHEN '8' MOVE '5'    TO PAXFEE-CALCAREA (7:1) 
CCLAUS*       WHEN '9' MOVE '5'    TO PAXFEE-CALCAREA (7:1) 
CCLAUS*    END-EVALUATE.
CCLAUS     EVALUATE PXFR-BOOK-NUM (6:1) 
CCLAUS        WHEN 'B' WHEN 'C' WHEN 'D' WHEN 'F' WHEN '0' WHEN '1' 
CCLAUS              MOVE '1'  TO PAXFEE-CALCAREA (7:1)  
CCLAUS        WHEN 'G' WHEN 'H' WHEN 'J' WHEN 'K' WHEN '2' WHEN '3' 
CCLAUS              MOVE '2'  TO PAXFEE-CALCAREA (7:1)  
CCLAUS        WHEN 'L' WHEN 'M' WHEN 'N' WHEN 'P' WHEN '4' WHEN '5' 
CCLAUS              MOVE '3'  TO PAXFEE-CALCAREA (7:1)  
CCLAUS        WHEN 'Q' WHEN 'R' WHEN 'S' WHEN 'T' WHEN '6' WHEN '7' 
CCLAUS              MOVE '4'  TO PAXFEE-CALCAREA (7:1)  
CCLAUS        WHEN 'V' WHEN 'W' WHEN 'X' WHEN 'Z' WHEN '8' WHEN '9' 
CCLAUS              MOVE '5'  TO PAXFEE-CALCAREA (7:1)  
CCLAUS     END-EVALUATE.
CCLAUS  

           FETCH PAX-FEE-REC RECORD 
                 ON ERROR GO TO 14900-FETCH-PXFR-ERROR. 

           MOVE FUNCTION LENGTH (PAX-FEE-REC) TO REC-LEN.   

           IF RSP06-PXFR-INFO OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) (1:REC-LEN) NOT = 
                 PAX-FEE-REC (1:REC-LEN)
              MOVE 114 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

       1340-UPDATE-PXFR-REC.
           IF WS-UPDATE-FLAG = 'D'  
              DELETE PAX-FEE-REC RECORD 
                     ON ERROR GO TO 15000-DELETE-PXFR-ERROR 
168921*       GO TO 1360-CHECK-ANAR-INFO.   
168921        GO TO 1420-CHECK-PIAR-INFO
168921     END-IF.  

           MOVE RSP06-PXFR-INFO OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) TO PAX-FEE-REC. 

           IF (RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW NOT = PXFR-BOOK-NUM) OR  
                 (RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) NOT =   
                 PXFR-PAX-SEQ-NUM)  
              MOVE 115 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

           IF WS-UPDATE-FLAG = 'S'  
              MOVE 'PAXFEE'     TO PAXFEE-CALCAREA  
CCLAUS*       EVALUATE IBR-BOOK-NUM (6:1)   
CCLAUS*          WHEN '0' MOVE '1'    TO PAXFEE-CALCAREA (7:1)  
CCLAUS*          WHEN '1' MOVE '1'    TO PAXFEE-CALCAREA (7:1)  
CCLAUS*          WHEN '2' MOVE '2'    TO PAXFEE-CALCAREA (7:1)  
CCLAUS*          WHEN '3' MOVE '2'    TO PAXFEE-CALCAREA (7:1)  
CCLAUS*          WHEN '4' MOVE '3'    TO PAXFEE-CALCAREA (7:1)  
CCLAUS*          WHEN '5' MOVE '3'    TO PAXFEE-CALCAREA (7:1)  
CCLAUS*          WHEN '6' MOVE '4'    TO PAXFEE-CALCAREA (7:1)  
CCLAUS*          WHEN '7' MOVE '4'    TO PAXFEE-CALCAREA (7:1)  
CCLAUS*          WHEN '8' MOVE '5'    TO PAXFEE-CALCAREA (7:1)  
CCLAUS*          WHEN '9' MOVE '5'    TO PAXFEE-CALCAREA (7:1)  
CCLAUS*       END-EVALUATE  
CCLAUS        EVALUATE PXFR-BOOK-NUM (6:1)  
CCLAUS           WHEN 'B' WHEN 'C' WHEN 'D' WHEN 'F' WHEN '0' WHEN '1'  
CCLAUS                 MOVE '1'  TO PAXFEE-CALCAREA (7:1)   
CCLAUS           WHEN 'G' WHEN 'H' WHEN 'J' WHEN 'K' WHEN '2' WHEN '3'  
CCLAUS                 MOVE '2'  TO PAXFEE-CALCAREA (7:1)   
CCLAUS           WHEN 'L' WHEN 'M' WHEN 'N' WHEN 'P' WHEN '4' WHEN '5'  
CCLAUS                 MOVE '3'  TO PAXFEE-CALCAREA (7:1)   
CCLAUS           WHEN 'Q' WHEN 'R' WHEN 'S' WHEN 'T' WHEN '6' WHEN '7'  
CCLAUS                 MOVE '4'  TO PAXFEE-CALCAREA (7:1)   
CCLAUS           WHEN 'V' WHEN 'W' WHEN 'X' WHEN 'Z' WHEN '8' WHEN '9'  
CCLAUS                 MOVE '5'  TO PAXFEE-CALCAREA (7:1)   
CCLAUS        END-EVALUATE  
              STORE PAX-FEE-REC 
                    ON ERROR GO TO 15100-STORE-PXFR-ERROR   
           ELSE 
              MODIFY PAX-FEE-REC RECORD 
                    ON ERROR GO TO 15200-MODIFY-PXFR-ERROR.

CCLAUS              MOVE '6'  TO PAXPHONE-CALCAREA (9:1)
CCLAUS        WHEN 'S' WHEN 'T' WHEN '7'
CCLAUS              MOVE '7'  TO PAXPHONE-CALCAREA (9:1)
CCLAUS        WHEN 'V' WHEN 'W' WHEN '8'
CCLAUS              MOVE '8'  TO PAXPHONE-CALCAREA (9:1)
CCLAUS        WHEN 'X' WHEN 'Z' WHEN '9'
CCLAUS              MOVE '9'  TO PAXPHONE-CALCAREA (9:1)

161361*=================================================================
       1540-CHECK-PIID-INFO.
           IF WS-PAX-UPDATE-CODE = 'M' OR 'C'   
              GO TO 1640-CHECK-PIDAR-INFO.  

           MOVE LOW-VALUES TO WS-PIID-PROCESS-FLAGS.

           IF WS-PAX-UPDATE-CODE = 'N'  
              IF (RSP06-PIID-READ-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) NOT = 'Y')
                 GO TO 1640-CHECK-PIDAR-INFO
              ELSE  
                 IF RSP06-PIID-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) = 'Y'  
                    MOVE 'S' TO WS-UPDATE-FLAG  
                    MOVE ZERO TO PIID-SUB   
                    GO TO 1620-STORE-PIID-REC   
                 ELSE   
                    GO TO 1640-CHECK-PIDAR-INFO.

           IF (RSP06-PIID-READ-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) NOT =
                 RSP06-PIID-READ-IND OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB))   
              MOVE 125 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

           IF (RSP06-PIID-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) = 'Y')  
              IF RSP06-PIID-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) = 'N' 
                 MOVE 'D' TO WS-UPDATE-FLAG 
                 GO TO 1560-GET-OLD-PIID
              ELSE  
                 IF RSP06-PIID-INFO OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) =   
                       RSP06-PIID-INFO OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB)  
                    GO TO 1640-CHECK-PIDAR-INFO 
                 ELSE   
                    MOVE 'U' TO WS-UPDATE-FLAG  
                    GO TO 1560-GET-OLD-PIID.

           IF RSP06-PIID-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) = 'Y'
              MOVE 'S'  TO WS-UPDATE-FLAG   
              MOVE ZERO TO PIID-SUB 
              GO TO 1620-STORE-PIID-REC 
           ELSE 
              GO TO 1640-CHECK-PIDAR-INFO.  

       1560-GET-OLD-PIID.   
           IF (RSP06-PIAR-READ-IND OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) NOT = 'Y') OR
                 (RSP06-PIAR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) NOT = 'Y')   
                 MOVE 126 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX   
                 GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG. 

           MOVE ZERO TO OPIID-SUB.  

       1580-NEXT-PIID-REC.  
           FETCH NEXT PAX-INFO-IMMIG-DOC-REC
                 WITHIN PAX-INFO-ADDR-IMMIG-SET SET 
                 ON ERROR GO TO 1600-CHECK-PIID-ERROR.  

           ADD 1 TO OPIID-SUB.  

           IF OPIID-SUB > RSP06-PIID-CNT OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB)
              MOVE 127 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

           IF RSP06-PIID-ENTRY OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB OPIID-SUB) NOT =  
                 PAX-INFO-IMMIG-DOC-REC 
              MOVE 128 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

           IF WS-UPDATE-FLAG = 'D' OR   
                 OPIID-SUB > RSP06-PIID-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) 
              DELETE PAX-INFO-IMMIG-DOC-REC RECORD  
                     ON ERROR GO TO 16500-DELETE-PIID-ERROR 
              GO TO 1580-NEXT-PIID-REC. 

120713*    MOVE 1 TO WS-PIID-PROCESSED-FLAG (NSUB). 
120713     MOVE 1 TO WS-PIID-PROCESSED-FLAG (OPIID-SUB).

           IF RSP06-PIID-ENTRY OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB OPIID-SUB) NOT =  
                 PAX-INFO-IMMIG-DOC-REC 
              MOVE RSP06-PIID-ENTRY OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB OPIID-SUB) TO
                   PAX-INFO-IMMIG-DOC-REC   
              MODIFY PAX-INFO-IMMIG-DOC-REC RECORD  
                     ON ERROR GO TO 16600-MODIFY-PIID-ERROR.

           GO TO 1580-NEXT-PIID-REC.

       1600-CHECK-PIID-ERROR.                                            I  
           IF (ERROR-NUM NOT = '0007' AND '0013')   
              MOVE 129 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
              GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR.

           IF OPIID-SUB NOT = RSP06-PIID-CNT OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB)
              MOVE 130 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

           MOVE ZERO TO PIID-SUB.   

       1620-STORE-PIID-REC. 
           ADD 1 TO PIID-SUB.   

           IF PIID-SUB > RSP06-PIID-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) 
              GO TO 1640-CHECK-PIDAR-INFO.  

           IF WS-PIID-PROCESSED-FLAG (PIID-SUB) = 1 
              GO TO 1620-STORE-PIID-REC.

           MOVE RSP06-PIID-ENTRY OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB PIID-SUB) TO
                PAX-INFO-IMMIG-DOC-REC. 

           STORE PAX-INFO-IMMIG-DOC-REC 
                 ON ERROR GO TO 16700-STORE-PIID-ERROR  

           GO TO 1620-STORE-PIID-REC.   

161361*=================================================================
       1640-CHECK-PIDAR-INFO.   
           IF WS-PAX-UPDATE-CODE = 'M' OR 'C'   
122022        GO TO 1780-CHECK-PICR-INFO.   

           IF WS-PAX-UPDATE-CODE = 'N'  
              IF (RSP06-PIDAR-READ-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) NOT = 'Y')   
122022           GO TO 1780-CHECK-PICR-INFO 
              ELSE  
                 IF RSP06-PIDAR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) = 'Y' 
                    MOVE 'S' TO WS-UPDATE-FLAG  
                    GO TO 1680-UPDATE-PIDAR-REC 
                 ELSE   
122022              GO TO 1780-CHECK-PICR-INFO. 

           IF (RSP06-PIDAR-READ-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) NOT =   
                 RSP06-PIDAR-READ-IND OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB))  
              MOVE 131 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

           IF (RSP06-PIDAR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) = 'Y') 
              IF RSP06-PIDAR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) = 'N'
                 MOVE 'D' TO WS-UPDATE-FLAG 
                 GO TO 1660-GET-OLD-PIDAR   
              ELSE  
                 IF RSP06-PIDAR-INFO OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) =  
                       RSP06-PIDAR-INFO OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) 
122022              GO TO 1780-CHECK-PICR-INFO  
                 ELSE   
                    MOVE 'U' TO WS-UPDATE-FLAG  
                    GO TO 1660-GET-OLD-PIDAR.   

           IF RSP06-PIDAR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) = 'Y'   
              MOVE 'S' TO WS-UPDATE-FLAG
              GO TO 1680-UPDATE-PIDAR-REC   
           ELSE 
122022        GO TO 1780-CHECK-PICR-INFO.   

       1660-GET-OLD-PIDAR.  
           IF (RSP06-PIAR-READ-IND OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) NOT = 'Y') OR
                 (RSP06-PIAR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) NOT = 'Y')   
                 MOVE 132 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX   
                 GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG. 

           FETCH FIRST PAX-INFO-DEST-ADDR-REC   
                 WITHIN PAX-INFO-ADDR-DADDR-SET SET 
                 ON ERROR GO TO 16800-FETCH-PIDAR-ERROR.

           IF RSP06-PIDAR-INFO OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) NOT =
                 PAX-INFO-DEST-ADDR-REC 
              MOVE 133 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

       1680-UPDATE-PIDAR-REC.   
           IF WS-UPDATE-FLAG = 'D'  
              DELETE PAX-INFO-DEST-ADDR-REC RECORD  
                     ON ERROR GO TO 16900-DELETE-PIDAR-ERROR
122022        GO TO 1780-CHECK-PICR-INFO.   

           MOVE RSP06-PIDAR-INFO OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) TO 
                PAX-INFO-DEST-ADDR-REC. 

           IF WS-UPDATE-FLAG = 'S'  
              STORE PAX-INFO-DEST-ADDR-REC  
                    ON ERROR GO TO 17000-STORE-PIDAR-ERROR  
           ELSE 
              MODIFY PAX-INFO-DEST-ADDR-REC RECORD  
                    ON ERROR GO TO 17100-MODIFY-PIDAR-ERROR.

122022 1780-CHECK-PICR-INFO.
           IF WS-PAX-UPDATE-CODE = 'M' OR 'C'   
              GO TO 1880-CHECK-PICC-INFO.   

122022     MOVE LOW-VALUES TO WS-PICR-PROCESS-FLAGS.

           IF WS-PAX-UPDATE-CODE = 'N'  
122022        IF (RSP06-PICR-READ-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) NOT = 'Y')
                 GO TO 1880-CHECK-PICC-INFO 
              ELSE  
122022           IF RSP06-PICR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) = 'Y'  
                    MOVE 'S' TO WS-UPDATE-FLAG  
122022              MOVE ZERO TO PICR-SUB   
122022              GO TO 1860-STORE-PICR-REC   
                 ELSE   
                    GO TO 1880-CHECK-PICC-INFO. 

122022     IF (RSP06-PICR-READ-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) NOT =
122022           RSP06-PICR-READ-IND OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB))   
              MOVE 140 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

122022     IF (RSP06-PICR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) = 'Y')  
122022        IF RSP06-PICR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) = 'N' 
                 MOVE 'D' TO WS-UPDATE-FLAG 
                 GO TO 1800-GET-OLD-PICR
              ELSE  
                 IF RSP06-PICR-INFO OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) =   
                       RSP06-PICR-INFO OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB)  
                    GO TO 1880-CHECK-PICC-INFO  
                 ELSE   
                    MOVE 'U' TO WS-UPDATE-FLAG  
                    GO TO 1800-GET-OLD-PICR.

122022     IF RSP06-PICR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) = 'Y'
              MOVE 'S' TO WS-UPDATE-FLAG
122022        MOVE ZERO TO PICR-SUB 
              GO TO 1860-STORE-PICR-REC 
           ELSE 
              GO TO 1880-CHECK-PICC-INFO.   

122022 1800-GET-OLD-PICR.   
122022     IF (RSP06-PICR-READ-IND OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) NOT = 'Y') OR
122022           (RSP06-PICR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) NOT = 'Y')   
122022           MOVE 175 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX   
122022           GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG. 

122022     MOVE ZERO TO OPICR-SUB.  

122022 1820-NEXT-PICR-REC.  
122022     FETCH NEXT PAX-INFO-CONTACT-REC  
122022           WITHIN PAX-INFO-ADDR-CONTACT-SET SET   
122022           ON ERROR GO TO 1840-CHECK-PICR-ERROR.  

122022     ADD 1 TO OPICR-SUB.  

122022     IF OPICR-SUB > RSP06-PICR-CNT OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB)
122022        MOVE 176 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
122022        GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

122022     IF RSP06-PICR-ENTRY OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB OPICR-SUB) NOT =  
122022           PAX-INFO-CONTACT-REC   
122022        MOVE 142 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
122022        GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

122022     IF WS-UPDATE-FLAG = 'D' OR   
122022           OPICR-SUB > RSP06-PICR-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) 
122022        DELETE PAX-INFO-CONTACT-REC RECORD
122022               ON ERROR GO TO 18100-DELETE-PICR-ERROR 
122022        GO TO 1820-NEXT-PICR-REC. 

122022     MOVE 1 TO WS-PICR-PROCESSED-FLAG (OPICR-SUB).

122022     IF RSP06-PICR-ENTRY OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB OPICR-SUB) NOT =  
122022           PAX-INFO-CONTACT-REC   
122022        MOVE RSP06-PICR-ENTRY OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB OPICR-SUB) TO
122022             PAX-INFO-CONTACT-REC 
122022        MODIFY PAX-INFO-CONTACT-REC RECORD
122022               ON ERROR GO TO 18300-MODIFY-PICR-ERROR.

122022     GO TO 1820-NEXT-PICR-REC.

122022 1840-CHECK-PICR-ERROR.   
122022     IF (ERROR-NUM NOT = '0007' AND '0013')   
122022        MOVE 177 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
122022        GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR.

122022     IF OPICR-SUB NOT = RSP06-PICR-CNT OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB)
122022        MOVE 178 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
122022        GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

122022     MOVE ZERO TO PICR-SUB.   

122022 1860-STORE-PICR-REC. 
122022     ADD 1 TO PICR-SUB.   

122022     IF PICR-SUB > RSP06-PICR-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) 
122022        GO TO 1880-CHECK-PICC-INFO.   

122022     IF WS-PICR-PROCESSED-FLAG (PICR-SUB) = 1 
122022        GO TO 1860-STORE-PICR-REC.

122022     MOVE RSP06-PICR-ENTRY OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB PICR-SUB) TO
122022          PAX-INFO-CONTACT-REC.   

122022     STORE PAX-INFO-CONTACT-REC   
122022           ON ERROR GO TO 18200-STORE-PICR-ERROR. 

122022     GO TO 1860-STORE-PICR-REC.

       1880-CHECK-PICC-INFO.
207898     IF  WS-PAX-UPDATE-CODE = 'C' 
207898         MOVE ZEROES             TO WS-PSUB

207898                   UNTIL WS-PSUB > RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-NEW 
207898         IF (RSP06-PICC-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (WS-PSUB) = 'Y')   
207898           IF RSP06-PICC-TERMS-ACCPT-BY-PSN OF RSP06-NEW (WS-PSUB)
207898                     = RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB)  
207898                MOVE 'D'         TO WS-UPDATE-FLAG
207898                PERFORM 8970-DELETE-PICC-ACCEPT-BY THRU 8970-EXIT 
207898           END-IF 
207898         END-IF   
207898         END-PERFORM  
207898         GO TO 1940-CHECK-PICF-INFO   
207898     END-IF.

              GO TO 1940-CHECK-PICF-INFO.   

           IF WS-PAX-UPDATE-CODE = 'N'  
              IF (RSP06-PICC-READ-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) NOT = 'Y')
                 GO TO 1940-CHECK-PICF-INFO

                    MOVE 'S' TO WS-UPDATE-FLAG  
                    GO TO 1920-UPDATE-PICC-REC  
                 ELSE   
                    GO TO 1940-CHECK-PICF-INFO.

                 RSP06-PICC-READ-IND OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB))   
              MOVE 143 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

           IF (RSP06-PICC-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) = 'Y')  
              IF RSP06-PICC-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) = 'N' 
                 MOVE 'D' TO WS-UPDATE-FLAG 
                 GO TO 1900-GET-OLD-PICC
              ELSE  
                 IF RSP06-PICC-INFO OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) =   
                       RSP06-PICC-INFO OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB)  
                    GO TO 1940-CHECK-PICF-INFO  
                 ELSE   
                    MOVE 'U' TO WS-UPDATE-FLAG  
                    GO TO 1900-GET-OLD-PICC.

           IF RSP06-PICC-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) = 'Y'
              MOVE 'S' TO WS-UPDATE-FLAG

              GO TO 1920-UPDATE-PICC-REC
           ELSE 
              GO TO 1940-CHECK-PICF-INFO.

                 (RSP06-PIAR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) NOT = 'Y')   
                 MOVE 144 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX

122022           WITHIN PAX-INFO-ADDR-CAMPC-SET SET 
                 ON ERROR GO TO 18400-FETCH-PICC-ERROR.

           IF RSP06-PICC-INFO OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) (1:REC-LEN) NOT =

       1940-CHECK-PICF-INFO.

           IF WS-PAX-UPDATE-CODE = 'N'

                 GO TO 2040-CHECK-PICH-INFO

                 GO TO 1960-GET-OLD-PICF
              ELSE

                       RSP06-PICF-INFO OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB)  
                    GO TO 2040-CHECK-PICH-INFO  
                 ELSE

              MOVE 'S'  TO WS-UPDATE-FLAG   
              MOVE ZERO TO PICF-SUB

                 (RSP06-PICF-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) NOT = 'Y')

                 WITHIN PAX-INFO-ADDR-FLIGHT-SET SET
                 ON ERROR GO TO 2000-CHECK-PICF-ERROR.  

           ADD 1 TO OPICF-SUB.  

           IF OPICF-SUB > RSP06-PICF-CNT OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB)
              MOVE 148 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX

           IF RSP06-PICF-REC OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB OPICF-SUB) (1:REC-LEN)  
                 NOT = PAX-INFO-COP-FLIGHT-REC (1:REC-LEN)  
              MOVE 149 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

                 OPICF-SUB > RSP06-PICF-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) 
              DELETE PAX-INFO-COP-FLIGHT-REC RECORD 
                     ON ERROR GO TO 18800-DELETE-PICF-ERROR 
              GO TO 1980-NEXT-PICF-REC.

           IF RSP06-PICF-REC OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB OPICF-SUB) NOT =
                 PAX-INFO-COP-FLIGHT-REC
              MOVE RSP06-PICF-REC OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB OPICF-SUB) TO  
                   PAX-INFO-COP-FLIGHT-REC  
              MODIFY PAX-INFO-COP-FLIGHT-REC RECORD 
                     ON ERROR GO TO 18900-MODIFY-PICF-ERROR.

              GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR.

       2020-STORE-PICF-REC.

           IF PICF-SUB > RSP06-PICF-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) 
              GO TO 2040-CHECK-PICH-INFO.   

           IF WS-PICF-PROCESSED-FLAG (PICF-SUB) = 1 
              GO TO 2020-STORE-PICF-REC.

           MOVE RSP06-PICF-REC OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB PICF-SUB) TO  
                PAX-INFO-COP-FLIGHT-REC.

           STORE PAX-INFO-COP-FLIGHT-REC
                 ON ERROR GO TO 19000-STORE-PICF-ERROR  

           GO TO 2020-STORE-PICF-REC.

                 IF RSP06-PICH-INFO OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) =   
                       RSP06-PICH-INFO OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB)  
                    GO TO 2140-CHECK-CSSXR-INFO 
                 ELSE   
                    MOVE 'U' TO WS-UPDATE-FLAG  
                    GO TO 2060-GET-OLD-PICH.

           IF RSP06-PICH-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) = 'Y'
              MOVE 'S'  TO WS-UPDATE-FLAG   
              MOVE ZERO TO PICH-SUB 
              GO TO 2120-STORE-PICH-REC 
           ELSE 
              GO TO 2140-CHECK-CSSXR-INFO.  

       2060-GET-OLD-PICH.   
           IF (RSP06-PICH-READ-IND OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) NOT = 'Y') OR
                 (RSP06-PICH-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) NOT = 'Y')   
                 MOVE 153 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX   
                 GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG. 

           MOVE ZERO TO OPICH-SUB.  

       2080-NEXT-PICH-REC.  
           FETCH NEXT PAX-INFO-COP-HOTEL-REC
                 WITHIN PAX-INFO-ADDR-HOTEL-SET SET 
                 ON ERROR GO TO 2100-CHECK-PICH-ERROR.  

           ADD 1 TO OPICH-SUB.  

           IF OPICH-SUB > RSP06-PICH-CNT OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB)
              MOVE 154 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

           IF RSP06-PICH-REC OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB OPICH-SUB) NOT =
                 PAX-INFO-COP-HOTEL-REC 
              MOVE 155 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

           IF WS-UPDATE-FLAG = 'D' OR   
                 OPICH-SUB > RSP06-PICH-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) 
              DELETE PAX-INFO-COP-HOTEL-REC RECORD  
                     ON ERROR GO TO 19100-DELETE-PICH-ERROR 
              GO TO 2080-NEXT-PICH-REC. 

119632     MOVE 1 TO WS-PICH-PROCESSED-FLAG (OPICH-SUB).

           IF RSP06-PICH-REC OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB OPICH-SUB) NOT =
                 PAX-INFO-COP-HOTEL-REC 
119632        MOVE RSP06-PICH-REC OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB OPICH-SUB) TO  
119632             PAX-INFO-COP-HOTEL-REC   
              MODIFY PAX-INFO-COP-HOTEL-REC RECORD  
                     ON ERROR GO TO 19200-MODIFY-PICH-ERROR.

           GO TO 2080-NEXT-PICH-REC.

       2100-CHECK-PICH-ERROR.   
           IF (ERROR-NUM NOT = '0007' AND '0013')   
              MOVE 156 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
              GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR.

           IF OPICH-SUB NOT = RSP06-PICH-CNT OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB)
              MOVE 157 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

           MOVE ZERO TO PICH-SUB.   

       2120-STORE-PICH-REC. 
           ADD 1 TO PICH-SUB.   

           IF PICH-SUB > RSP06-PICH-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) 
              GO TO 2140-CHECK-CSSXR-INFO.  

           IF WS-PICH-PROCESSED-FLAG (PICH-SUB) = 1 
              GO TO 2120-STORE-PICH-REC.

           MOVE RSP06-PICH-REC OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB PICH-SUB) TO  
                PAX-INFO-COP-HOTEL-REC. 

           STORE PAX-INFO-COP-HOTEL-REC 
                 ON ERROR GO TO 19300-STORE-PICH-ERROR  

           GO TO 2120-STORE-PICH-REC.

              GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR.

           IF OPPR-SUB NOT = RSP06-PPR-CNT OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB)  
              MOVE 202 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

           IF PPR-SUB = ZERO
              GO TO 2360-CHECK-PPIR-INFO.   

125001     IF RSP06-PPR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) = 'N' 
125001        GO TO 2360-CHECK-PPIR-INFO.   

           MOVE ZERO TO PPR-SUB.

       2340-STORE-PPR-REC.  
           ADD 1 TO PPR-SUB.

           IF PPR-SUB > RSP06-PPR-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB)   
              GO TO 2360-CHECK-PPIR-INFO.   

           IF WS-PPR-PROCESSED-FLAG (PPR-SUB) = 1   
              GO TO 2340-STORE-PPR-REC. 

           MOVE RSP06-PPR-TPR-SUB OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB PPR-SUB) TO
                TPR-SUB.
           MOVE RSP06-PPR-TPR-PAX-SUB OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB PPR-SUB) TO
                PSUB.   

           IF (RSP06-PPR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB PPR-SUB) NOT = 
                 RSP06-PAX-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB)) OR 
                 (RSP06-PPR-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB PPR-SUB) 
                 NOT = RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB))   
              MOVE 203 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

           IF (RSP06-PPR-PKG-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB PPR-SUB) NOT =  
                 RSP06-TPR-PKG-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (TPR-SUB)) OR   
                 (RSP06-PAX-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) NOT =  
                 RSP06-TPR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (TPR-SUB PSUB)) OR 
                 (RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) NOT =   
                 RSP06-TPR-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (TPR-SUB PSUB)) OR  
                 (RSP06-PAX-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) NOT = 
                 RSP06-TPR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (TPR-SUB PSUB))   
              MOVE 204 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

           MOVE RSP06-PPR-REC OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB PPR-SUB) TO
                PAX-PKG-REC.

           STORE PAX-PKG-REC
                 ON ERROR GO TO 20400-STORE-PPR-ERROR   

           GO TO 2340-STORE-PPR-REC.

       2360-CHECK-PPIR-INFO.

           IF WS-PAX-UPDATE-CODE = 'M'  
113068        GO TO 2500-CHECK-CIF-ENTRY.   

           IF WS-PAX-UPDATE-CODE = 'C'  
              IF (RSP06-PPIR-READ-IND OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) NOT = 'Y')
                 MOVE 205 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX   
                 GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG  
              ELSE  
                 IF RSP06-PPIR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) NOT = 'Y'  
113068              GO TO 2500-CHECK-CIF-ENTRY  
                 ELSE   
                    MOVE 'D' TO WS-UPDATE-FLAG

           IF WS-PAX-UPDATE-CODE = 'N'  
              IF (RSP06-PPIR-READ-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) NOT = 'Y')
113068           GO TO 2500-CHECK-CIF-ENTRY 
              ELSE  
                 IF RSP06-PPIR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) = 'Y'  
                    IF RSP06-PPIR-ENTRY-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) > 0 
                       MOVE 'S' TO WS-UPDATE-FLAG   
                       GO TO 2400-UPDATE-PPIR-REC   
                    ELSE
                       MOVE 206 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX 
                       GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG
                 ELSE   
113068              GO TO 2500-CHECK-CIF-ENTRY. 

           IF (RSP06-PPIR-READ-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) NOT =
                 RSP06-PPIR-READ-IND OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB))   
              MOVE 207 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

           IF (RSP06-PPIR-READ-IND OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) NOT = 'Y')   
              IF (RSP06-PAX-CANCEL-DATE OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) NOT =   
                    PAX-CANCEL-DATE)
                 MOVE 208 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX   
                 GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG. 

           IF PAX-CANCEL-DATE > ZERO
              IF RSP06-PPIR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) = 'Y' 
                 MOVE 209 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX   
                 GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG. 
171943     IF WS-SAIL-CHG-FLAG = 'Y'
171943        IF RSP06-PPIR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) = 'Y' AND 
171943           RSP06-PPIR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) = 'Y' 
171943           PERFORM 8040-UPDATE-TARGET-RED THRU 8040-EXIT  
171943        END-IF
171943     END-IF.  

           IF (RSP06-PPIR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) = 'Y')  
              IF RSP06-PPIR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) = 'N' OR  
                    RSP06-PPIR-ENTRY-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) = ZERO 
                 MOVE 'D' TO WS-UPDATE-FLAG 
                 GO TO 2380-GET-OLD-PPIR
              ELSE  
                 IF RSP06-PPIR-INFO OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) =   
                       RSP06-PPIR-INFO OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) AND  
                       RSP06-PAX-PAST-GUEST-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) =   
                       RSP06-PAX-PAST-GUEST-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) 
113068              GO TO 2500-CHECK-CIF-ENTRY  
                 ELSE   
                    MOVE 'U' TO WS-UPDATE-FLAG  
                    GO TO 2380-GET-OLD-PPIR.

           IF RSP06-PPIR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) = 'Y'
              IF RSP06-PPIR-ENTRY-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) > ZERO
                 MOVE 'S' TO WS-UPDATE-FLAG 
                 GO TO 2400-UPDATE-PPIR-REC.

113068     GO TO 2500-CHECK-CIF-ENTRY.  

       2380-GET-OLD-PPIR.   
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-OLD  TO PPIR-BOOK-NUM.  
           MOVE RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) TO
                PPIR-PAX-SEQ-NUM.   

           FETCH PAX-PROMO-ITEM-REC RECORD  
                 ON ERROR GO TO 28900-FETCH-PPIR-ERROR. 

           MOVE FUNCTION LENGTH (PAX-PROMO-ITEM-REC) TO REC-LEN.

           IF RSP06-PPIR-INFO OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) (1:REC-LEN) NOT = 
                 PAX-PROMO-ITEM-REC (1:REC-LEN) 
              MOVE 210 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

       2400-UPDATE-PPIR-REC.
           IF WS-UPDATE-FLAG = 'D'  
              DELETE PAX-PROMO-ITEM-REC RECORD  
                     ON ERROR GO TO 29000-DELETE-PPIR-ERROR 
              GO TO 2420-UPDATE-TARGET-OFFERS.  

           MOVE RSP06-PPIR-INFO OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) TO  
                PAX-PROMO-ITEM-REC. 

           IF (RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW NOT = PPIR-BOOK-NUM) OR  
                 (RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) NOT =   
                 PPIR-PAX-SEQ-NUM)  
              MOVE 211 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

           IF WS-UPDATE-FLAG = 'S'  
              STORE PAX-PROMO-ITEM-REC  
                    ON ERROR GO TO 29100-STORE-PPIR-ERROR   
           ELSE 
              MODIFY PAX-PROMO-ITEM-REC RECORD  
                    ON ERROR GO TO 29200-MODIFY-PPIR-ERROR. 

       2420-UPDATE-TARGET-OFFERS.   
            IF WS-UPDATE-FLAG = 'S' 
               GO TO 2460-UPDATE-NEW-PPIRS. 

            MOVE ZERO TO OPPIR-SUB. 

       2440-NEXT-OLD-PPIR-ENTRY.
           ADD 1 TO OPPIR-SUB.  

           IF OPPIR-SUB > RSP06-PPIR-ENTRY-CNT OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB)  
              GO TO 2460-UPDATE-NEW-PPIRS.  

           MOVE 'U' TO WS-OFFER-ACTION-CODE.

           IF WS-UPDATE-FLAG NOT = 'D'  
              MOVE ZERO TO PPIR-SUB 
              PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
                    UNTIL SUB > RSP06-PPIR-ENTRY-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB)
                 IF RSP06-PPIR-OFFER-ID OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB OPPIR-SUB) = 
                       RSP06-PPIR-OFFER-ID OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB SUB)  
                    MOVE SUB TO PPIR-SUB
                    MOVE RSP06-PPIR-ENTRY-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) TO
                         SUB
                 END-IF 
              END-PERFORM   
              IF PPIR-SUB > ZERO
                 IF RSP06-PAX-PAST-GUEST-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) =  
                       RSP06-PAX-PAST-GUEST-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) 
                    GO TO 2440-NEXT-OLD-PPIR-ENTRY  
                 ELSE   
                    MOVE 'E' TO WS-OFFER-ACTION-CODE.   

           PERFORM 8030-UPDATE-OFFER THRU 8039-EXIT.

           GO TO 2440-NEXT-OLD-PPIR-ENTRY.  

       2460-UPDATE-NEW-PPIRS.   
           IF WS-UPDATE-FLAG = 'D'  
113068        GO TO 2500-CHECK-CIF-ENTRY.   

121304     IF RSP06-PPIR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB) = 'N'
113068        GO TO 2500-CHECK-CIF-ENTRY.   

           MOVE ZERO TO PPIR-SUB.   

       2480-NEXT-NEW-PPIR-ENTRY.
           ADD 1 TO PPIR-SUB.   

           IF PPIR-SUB > RSP06-PPIR-ENTRY-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB)   
113068        GO TO 2500-CHECK-CIF-ENTRY.   

           IF WS-UPDATE-FLAG NOT = 'S'  
              MOVE ZERO TO OPPIR-SUB
              PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
                    UNTIL SUB > RSP06-PPIR-ENTRY-CNT OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB)
                 IF RSP06-PPIR-OFFER-ID OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB PPIR-SUB) =  
                       RSP06-PPIR-OFFER-ID OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB SUB)  
                    MOVE SUB TO OPPIR-SUB   
                    MOVE RSP06-PPIR-ENTRY-CNT OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) TO
                         SUB
                 END-IF 
              END-PERFORM   
              IF OPPIR-SUB > ZERO   
                 GO TO 2480-NEXT-NEW-PPIR-ENTRY.

           MOVE 'R' TO WS-OFFER-ACTION-CODE.

           PERFORM 8030-UPDATE-OFFER THRU 8039-EXIT.

           GO TO 2480-NEXT-NEW-PPIR-ENTRY.

           MOVE RSP06-TPR-REC OF RSP06-NEW (TPR-SUB) TO 
                TOUR-PACKAGE-REC.

                  ON ERROR GO TO 20700-MODIFY-TPR-ERROR.

       3040-STORE-NEW-TPRS.

                   UNTIL TPR-SUB > RSP06-TPR-CNT OF RSP06-NEW

                      TOUR-PACKAGE-REC

           END-PERFORM.

PPC470*       FIND CURRENT SAIL1-REC RECORD 
PPC470*            ON ERROR GO TO 28500-FIND-SAIL1-ERROR
PPC470*       MODIFY SAIL1-REC RECORD   
PPC470*              ON ERROR GO TO 28400-MODIFY-SAIL1-ERROR.   
PPC470        PERFORM VARYING DIN-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
PPC470                  UNTIL DIN-SUB > 6 OR
PPC470                        TDP01-DINING-TIME-CODE(DIN-SUB) <= SPACES 
PPC470           EXEC SQL   
PPC470                UPDATE  SAIL_DINING   
PPC470                   SET  GUESTS_ASSIGNED_CNT  =
PPC470                       :TDP01-GUESTS-ASSIGNED-CNT(DIN-SUB),   
PPC470                        GUESTS_WAIT_CNT  =
PPC470                       :TDP01-GUESTS-WAIT-CNT(DIN-SUB)
PPC470                 WHERE  SHIP_CODE = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE  
PPC470                   AND  SAIL_ID = :TDP01-SAIL-ID  
PPC470                   AND  DINING_TIME_CODE =
PPC470                       :TDP01-DINING-TIME-CODE(DIN-SUB)   
PPC470                   AND (GUESTS_ASSIGNED_CNT <>
PPC470                       :TDP01-GUESTS-ASSIGNED-CNT(DIN-SUB)
PPC470                    OR  GUESTS_WAIT_CNT  <>   
PPC470                       :TDP01-GUESTS-WAIT-CNT(DIN-SUB))   
PPC470           END-EXEC   
PPC470           IF SQLCODE < 0 
PPC470              GO TO 28400-MODIFY-SAIL1-ERROR  
PPC470           END-IF 
PPC470        END-PERFORM   
PPC470     END-IF.

              IF (GBR1-DINING-TIME-CODE (DIN-SUB) NOT = WS-NEW-DIN-CNF)

              IF WS-OLD-DIN-PAX-CNT > GBR1-DINING-BTH-PAX-CNT (DIN-SUB) 
                 MOVE ZERO   TO GBR1-DINING-BTH-PAX-CNT (DIN-SUB)   
              ELSE  
                 SUBTRACT WS-OLD-DIN-PAX-CNT FROM   
                          GBR1-DINING-BTH-PAX-CNT (DIN-SUB) 
                          GIVING N18-SIGNED 
                 MOVE N18-UNSIGNED TO   
                      GBR1-DINING-BTH-PAX-CNT (DIN-SUB) 
              END-IF
              MOVE 'Y'    TO WS-UPDATE-IND  
              MOVE SPACES TO WS-OLD-DIN-CNF 
              MOVE ZERO   TO WS-OLD-DIN-PAX-CNT 
              IF WS-NEW-DIN-PAX-CNT = ZERO  
                 GO TO 3600-CHECK-NBK-FLAG  
              ELSE  
                 GO TO 3580-NEXT-GRP-DINING.

           COMPUTE WS-DIN-ADJ-CNT = WS-NEW-DIN-PAX-CNT  
                                  - WS-OLD-DIN-PAX-CNT. 

           IF WS-DIN-ADJ-CNT = ZERO 
              GO TO 3600-CHECK-NBK-FLAG.

           IF WS-NEW-DIN-PAX-CNT > WS-OLD-DIN-PAX-CNT   
              ADD WS-DIN-ADJ-CNT TO GBR1-DINING-BTH-PAX-CNT (DIN-SUB)

              MOVE 'Y'    TO WS-UPDATE-IND  
              MOVE SPACES TO WS-NEW-DIN-CNF 
                             WS-OLD-DIN-CNF 
              MOVE ZERO   TO WS-NEW-DIN-PAX-CNT 
                             WS-OLD-DIN-PAX-CNT

                          MOVE 'Y'  TO WS-UPDATE-IND

                           - RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-CASH-PMT OF RSP06-OLD  
              COMPUTE GBR1-UNALLOC-CASH-PMT-AMT =   
                         GBR1-UNALLOC-CASH-PMT-AMT  
                        - RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-CASH-PMT OF RSP06-NEW 
                        + RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-CASH-PMT OF RSP06-OLD.

              COMPUTE GBR1-UNALLOC-UCC-PMT-AMT =
                         GBR1-UNALLOC-UCC-PMT-AMT   
                        - RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-UCC-PMT OF RSP06-NEW
