               RSP06-IBR-CABIN-UCC-PMT OF RSP06-OLD 
               RSP06-IBR-CABIN-OTHER-PMT OF RSP06-OLD   
               RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-CASH-PMT OF RSP06-OLD
               RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-UCC-PMT OF RSP06-OLD 
               RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-OTHER-PMT OF RSP06-OLD   
               GIVING WS-OLD-TOT-PMT-AMT.   

           MOVE ZERO TO WS-OLD-DEP-AMT  
                        WS-NEW-DEP-AMT  
                        WS-OLD-DEP-PMT-AMT  
                        WS-NEW-DEP-PMT-AMT. 

           IF RSP06-IBR-CANCEL-DATE OF RSP06-OLD > ZERO 
              MOVE RSP06-IBR-NET-FARE-AMT OF RSP06-OLD TO WS-OLD-DEP-AMT
              IF RSP06-IBR-NET-FARE-AMT OF RSP06-OLD >  
                    WS-OLD-TOT-PMT-AMT  
                 MOVE WS-OLD-TOT-PMT-AMT  TO WS-OLD-DEP-PMT-AMT 
              ELSE  
                 MOVE RSP06-IBR-NET-FARE-AMT OF RSP06-OLD TO
                      WS-OLD-DEP-PMT-AMT
           ELSE 
              MOVE RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-AMT OF RSP06-OLD TO WS-OLD-DEP-AMT 
              IF RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-AMT OF RSP06-OLD >   
                    WS-OLD-TOT-PMT-AMT  
                 MOVE WS-OLD-TOT-PMT-AMT TO WS-OLD-DEP-PMT-AMT  
              ELSE  
                 MOVE RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-AMT OF RSP06-OLD TO 
                      WS-OLD-DEP-PMT-AMT.   

           IF RSP06-IBR-CANCEL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW > ZERO 
              MOVE RSP06-IBR-NET-FARE-AMT OF RSP06-NEW TO WS-NEW-DEP-AMT
              IF RSP06-IBR-NET-FARE-AMT OF RSP06-NEW > WS-TOT-PMT-AMT   
                 MOVE WS-TOT-PMT-AMT  TO WS-NEW-DEP-PMT-AMT 
              ELSE  
                 MOVE RSP06-IBR-NET-FARE-AMT OF RSP06-NEW TO
                      WS-NEW-DEP-PMT-AMT
           ELSE 
              MOVE RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-AMT OF RSP06-NEW TO WS-NEW-DEP-AMT 
              IF RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-AMT OF RSP06-NEW > WS-TOT-PMT-AMT
                 MOVE WS-TOT-PMT-AMT TO WS-NEW-DEP-PMT-AMT  
              ELSE  
                 MOVE RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-AMT OF RSP06-NEW TO 
                      WS-NEW-DEP-PMT-AMT.   

           IF (WS-OLD-DEP-AMT NOT = WS-NEW-DEP-AMT) 
              MOVE 'Y' TO WS-UPDATE-IND 
              COMPUTE GBR1-BTH-DEPOSIT-AMT = GBR1-BTH-DEPOSIT-AMT   
                                           - WS-OLD-DEP-AMT 
                                           + WS-NEW-DEP-AMT.

           IF (WS-OLD-DEP-PMT-AMT NOT = WS-NEW-DEP-PMT-AMT) 
              MOVE 'Y' TO WS-UPDATE-IND 
              COMPUTE GBR1-BTH-DEPOSIT-PAYMENT-AMT =
                                          GBR1-BTH-DEPOSIT-PAYMENT-AMT  
                                        - WS-OLD-DEP-PMT-AMT
                                        + WS-NEW-DEP-PMT-AMT.   

           IF WS-UPDATE-IND = 'Y'   
              GO TO 3640-MODIFY-GBR1
           ELSE 
              GO TO 3660-UPDATE-GROUP-FEES.

CCLAUS     ADD RSP06-IBR-COMM-GST-PAID-AMT OF RSP06-NEW  TO 
CCLAUS         GBR1-COMM-GST-PAID-AMT.  
CCLAUS     ADD RSP06-IBR-COMM-GST-RECLAIM-AMT OF RSP06-NEW  TO  
CCLAUS         GBR1-COMM-GST-RECLAIM-AMT.   
CCLAUS     ADD RSP06-IBR-CRUO-COMM-GST-AMT  OF RSP06-NEW  TO
CCLAUS         GBR1-CRUO-COMM-GST-AMT.  
CCLAUS     ADD RSP06-IBR-TRANS-COMM-GST-AMT OF RSP06-NEW  TO
CCLAUS         GBR1-TRANS-COMM-GST-AMT. 
CCLAUS     ADD RSP06-IBR-PEN-COMM-GST-AMT   OF RSP06-NEW  TO
CCLAUS         GBR1-PEN-COMM-GST-AMT.   
CCLAUS     ADD RSP06-IBR-PKG-COMM-GST-AMT   OF RSP06-NEW  TO
CCLAUS         GBR1-PKG-COMM-GST-AMT.   
CCLAUS     ADD RSP06-IBR-ADDON-COMM-GST-AMT OF RSP06-NEW  TO
CCLAUS         GBR1-ADDON-COMM-GST-AMT. 
PPC470*    ADD RSP06-IBR-PRIVATE-BUS-AMT OF RSP06-NEW TO
PPC470*        GBR1-PRIVATE-BUS-AMT.
           ADD RSP06-IBR-TOTAL-FEES-AMT OF RSP06-NEW TO 
               GBR1-TOTAL-FEES-AMT. 
           ADD RSP06-IBR-REFUND-AMT OF RSP06-NEW TO 
               GBR1-TOTAL-IBR-REFUND-AMT.   
           ADD RSP06-IBR-PROGRAM-FEE-AMT OF RSP06-NEW TO
               GBR1-TOTAL-PROGRAM-FEE-AMT.  
           ADD RSP06-IBR-CABIN-CASH-PMT OF RSP06-NEW TO 
               GBR1-CABIN-CASH-PMT-AMT. 
           ADD RSP06-IBR-CABIN-UCC-PMT OF RSP06-NEW TO  
               GBR1-CABIN-UCC-PMT-AMT.  
           ADD RSP06-IBR-CABIN-OTHER-PMT OF RSP06-NEW TO
               GBR1-CABIN-OTHER-PMT-AMT.
           ADD RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-CASH-PMT OF RSP06-NEW TO 
               GBR1-ALLOC-CASH-PMT-AMT. 
           ADD RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-UCC-PMT OF RSP06-NEW TO  
               GBR1-ALLOC-UCC-PMT-AMT.  
           ADD RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-OTHER-PMT OF RSP06-NEW TO
               GBR1-ALLOC-OTHER-PMT-AMT.

           IF RSP06-IBR-CANCEL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW > ZERO 
              ADD RSP06-IBR-NET-FARE-AMT OF RSP06-NEW TO
                  GBR1-BTH-DEPOSIT-AMT  
              IF RSP06-IBR-NET-FARE-AMT OF RSP06-NEW > WS-TOT-PMT-AMT   
                 ADD WS-TOT-PMT-AMT TO GBR1-BTH-DEPOSIT-PAYMENT-AMT 
              ELSE  
                 ADD RSP06-IBR-NET-FARE-AMT OF RSP06-NEW TO 
                     GBR1-BTH-DEPOSIT-PAYMENT-AMT   
           ELSE 
              ADD RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-AMT OF RSP06-NEW TO 
                  GBR1-BTH-DEPOSIT-AMT  
              IF (RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-AMT OF RSP06-NEW > WS-TOT-PMT-AMT)  
                 ADD WS-TOT-PMT-AMT TO GBR1-BTH-DEPOSIT-PAYMENT-AMT 
              ELSE  
                 ADD RSP06-IBR-DEPOSIT-AMT OF RSP06-NEW TO  
                     GBR1-BTH-DEPOSIT-PAYMENT-AMT.  

           SUBTRACT RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-CASH-PMT OF RSP06-NEW   
               FROM GBR1-UNALLOC-CASH-PMT-AMT.  
           SUBTRACT RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-UCC-PMT OF RSP06-NEW
               FROM GBR1-UNALLOC-UCC-PMT-AMT.   
           SUBTRACT RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-OTHER-PMT OF RSP06-NEW  
               FROM GBR1-UNALLOC-OTHER-PMT-AMT. 

       3640-MODIFY-GBR1.
           MOVE DPP02-SYSTEM-DATETIME TO GBR1-UPDATE-DATETIME.  

           FIND CURRENT GRP1-BOOKING-REC RECORD 
                ON ERROR GO TO 23700-FIND-GBR1-ERROR.   

           MODIFY GRP1-BOOKING-REC RECORD   
                  ON ERROR GO TO 23800-MODIFY-GBR1-ERROR.   
214641     MOVE ZEROES                TO WS-UPDATE-DATE-TIME.   
214641     MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE(1:16) 
214641                                TO WS-UPDATE-DATE-TIME.   
214641     MOVE WS-UPDATE-DATE-TIME(1:4)
214641                                TO WS-UPDATE-YEAR.
214641     MOVE WS-UPDATE-DATE-TIME(5:2)
214641                                TO WS-UPDATE-MONTH.   
214641     MOVE WS-UPDATE-DATE-TIME(7:2)
214641                                TO WS-UPDATE-DAY-DATE.
214641     MOVE WS-UPDATE-DATE-TIME(9:2)
214641                                TO WS-UPDATE-HOUR.
214641     MOVE WS-UPDATE-DATE-TIME(11:2)   
214641                                TO WS-UPDATE-MINUTE.  
214641     MOVE WS-UPDATE-DATE-TIME(13:2)   
214641                                TO WS-UPDATE-SECOND.  
214641     MOVE WS-UPDATE-DATE-TIME(15:2)   
214641                                TO WS-UPDATE-PRECISION.   
214641     MOVE '-'                   TO WS-UPDATE-HYPHEN-1 
214641                                   WS-UPDATE-HYPHEN-2.
214641     MOVE SPACES                TO WS-UPDATE-SPACE-1. 
214641     MOVE ':'                   TO WS-UPDATE-COLON-1  
214641                                   WS-UPDATE-COLON-2. 
214641     MOVE '.'                   TO WS-UPDATE-DOT. 

214641     MOVE ZEROES                TO WS-TOTAL-UNALLOC-PMT-AMT   
214641                                   WS-TOTAL-ALLOC-PMT-AMT.

214641     COMPUTE WS-TOTAL-UNALLOC-PMT-AMT = GBR1-UNALLOC-CASH-PMT-AMT 
214641                                      + GBR1-UNALLOC-UCC-PMT-AMT  
214641                                     + GBR1-UNALLOC-OTHER-PMT-AMT.

214641     COMPUTE WS-TOTAL-ALLOC-PMT-AMT = GBR1-ALLOC-CASH-PMT-AMT 
214641                                    + GBR1-ALLOC-UCC-PMT-AMT  
214641                                    + GBR1-ALLOC-OTHER-PMT-AMT.   

214641     IF WS-TOTAL-UNALLOC-PMT-AMT NOT = ZEROES 
214641        MOVE ZEROES             TO WS-TOTAL-UNALLOC-PMT-AMT-NI
214641     ELSE 
214641        MOVE -1                 TO WS-TOTAL-UNALLOC-PMT-AMT-NI
214641     END-IF.  

214641     IF WS-TOTAL-ALLOC-PMT-AMT NOT = ZEROES   
214641        MOVE ZEROES             TO WS-TOTAL-ALLOC-PMT-AMT-NI  
214641     ELSE 
214641        MOVE -1                 TO WS-TOTAL-ALLOC-PMT-AMT-NI  
214641     END-IF.  

214641     EXEC SQL UPDATE GROUP_SUMMARY
214641          SET (GSR_TOTAL_UNALLOC_PMT_AMT  
214641                                   = :WS-TOTAL-UNALLOC-PMT-AMT
214641                                     :WS-TOTAL-UNALLOC-PMT-AMT-NI,
214641               GSR_TOTAL_ALLOC_PMT_AMT
214641                                   = :WS-TOTAL-ALLOC-PMT-AMT  
214641                                     :WS-TOTAL-ALLOC-PMT-AMT-NI,  
214641               GSR_UPDATE_DATETIME = :WS-UPDATE-DATETIME) 
214641              WHERE GSR_BOOK_NUM = :GBR1-BOOK-NUM AND 
214641                   (GSR_UPDATE_DATETIME <> :WS-UPDATE-DATETIME OR 
214641                   ((:WS-TOTAL-UNALLOC-PMT-AMT-NI = -1         AND
214641                   GSR_TOTAL_UNALLOC_PMT_AMT IS NOT NULL)      OR 
214641                   (:WS-TOTAL-UNALLOC-PMT-AMT-NI = 0           AND
214641                   GSR_TOTAL_UNALLOC_PMT_AMT IS NULL)          OR 
214641                   GSR_TOTAL_UNALLOC_PMT_AMT <>   
214641                     :WS-TOTAL-UNALLOC-PMT-AMT)                OR 
214641                   ((:WS-TOTAL-ALLOC-PMT-AMT-NI = -1           AND
214641                   GSR_TOTAL_ALLOC_PMT_AMT IS NOT NULL)        OR 
214641                   (:WS-TOTAL-ALLOC-PMT-AMT-NI = 0             AND
214641                   GSR_TOTAL_ALLOC_PMT_AMT IS NULL)            OR 
214641                   GSR_TOTAL_ALLOC_PMT_AMT <> 
214641                     :WS-TOTAL-ALLOC-PMT-AMT))
214641     END-EXEC.

214641     IF SQLCODE < 0   
214641        GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
214641     END-IF.  

161361*=================================================================
       3660-UPDATE-GROUP-FEES.  
           MOVE 'N'           TO WS-UPDATE-IND. 
           MOVE GBR1-BOOK-NUM TO GFR1-BOOK-NUM. 

           FETCH GRP1-FEE-REC RECORD
                 ON ERROR GO TO 3680-INIT-FEE-RECORD.   

           MOVE 'N' TO WS-STORE-IND.

           GO TO 3700-WALK-FEE-RECORDS. 

       3680-INIT-FEE-RECORD.
           MOVE LOW-VALUES    TO GRP1-FEE-REC.  
           MOVE GBR1-BOOK-NUM TO GFR1-BOOK-NUM  
           MOVE 'Y'           TO WS-STORE-IND.  

       3700-WALK-FEE-RECORDS.   
           MOVE ZERO TO FEE-SUB.

       3720-ADD-NEXT-FEE.   
           ADD 1 TO FEE-SUB.

           IF FEE-SUB > RSP06-IFR-ENTRY-CTR OF RSP06-NEW
              GO TO 3760-CHECK-OLD-FEES.

           IF WS-NEW-BKG-FLAG = 'Y' 
              GO TO 3740-FIND-GRP-FEE.  

           MOVE ZERO TO OFEE-SUB.   

           PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
                   UNTIL SUB > RSP06-IFR-ENTRY-CTR OF RSP06-OLD 
              IF RSP06-IFR-FEE-CODE OF RSP06-OLD (SUB) =
                    RSP06-IFR-FEE-CODE OF RSP06-NEW (FEE-SUB)   
                 MOVE SUB TO OFEE-SUB   
                 MOVE RSP06-IFR-ENTRY-CTR OF RSP06-OLD TO SUB   
              END-IF
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF OFEE-SUB > ZERO   
              IF RSP06-IFR-FEE-TOTAL-AMT OF RSP06-NEW (FEE-SUB) =   
                    RSP06-IFR-FEE-TOTAL-AMT OF RSP06-OLD (OFEE-SUB) 
                 GO TO 3720-ADD-NEXT-FEE.   

       3740-FIND-GRP-FEE.   
           MOVE 'Y'  TO WS-UPDATE-IND.  
           MOVE ZERO TO GFEE-SUB.   

           PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
                   UNTIL SUB > GFR1-FEE-ENTRY-CNT   
              IF RSP06-IFR-FEE-CODE OF RSP06-NEW (FEE-SUB) =
                    GFR1-FEE-CODE (SUB) 
                 MOVE SUB                TO GFEE-SUB
                 MOVE GFR1-FEE-ENTRY-CNT TO SUB 
              END-IF
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF GFEE-SUB = ZERO   
              ADD 1 TO GFR1-FEE-ENTRY-CNT   
              MOVE LOW-VALUES TO GFR1-FEE-TABLE (GFR1-FEE-ENTRY-CNT)
              MOVE RSP06-IFR-FEE-CODE OF RSP06-NEW (FEE-SUB) TO 
                   GFR1-FEE-CODE (GFR1-FEE-ENTRY-CNT)   
              MOVE RSP06-IFR-FEE-GROUP-CODE OF RSP06-NEW (FEE-SUB) TO   
                   GFR1-FEE-GROUP-CODE (GFR1-FEE-ENTRY-CNT) 
              MOVE RSP06-IFR-FEE-TOTAL-AMT OF RSP06-NEW (FEE-SUB) TO
                   GFR1-FEE-TOTAL-AMT (GFR1-FEE-ENTRY-CNT)  
              MOVE RSP06-IFR-FEE-SPECIAL-IND OF RSP06-NEW (FEE-SUB) TO  
                   GFR1-SPECIAL-IND (GFR1-FEE-ENTRY-CNT)
CALFEE        MOVE RSP06-IFR-GOVT-FEE-IND OF RSP06-NEW (FEE-SUB) TO 
CALFEE             GFR1-GOVT-FEE-IND  (GFR1-FEE-ENTRY-CNT)  
CALFEE        MOVE RSP06-IFR-INCL-IN-CRS-FARE-IND OF RSP06-NEW  
CALFEE                                                 (FEE-SUB) TO 
CALFEE             GFR1-INCL-IN-CRS-FARE-IND (GFR1-FEE-ENTRY-CNT)   
              GO TO 3720-ADD-NEXT-FEE.  

           IF OFEE-SUB = ZERO   
              ADD RSP06-IFR-FEE-TOTAL-AMT OF RSP06-NEW (FEE-SUB) TO 
                  GFR1-FEE-TOTAL-AMT (GFEE-SUB) 
              GO TO 3720-ADD-NEXT-FEE.  

           COMPUTE GFR1-FEE-TOTAL-AMT (GFEE-SUB) =  
                        GFR1-FEE-TOTAL-AMT (GFEE-SUB)   
                      + RSP06-IFR-FEE-TOTAL-AMT OF RSP06-NEW (FEE-SUB)  
                      - RSP06-IFR-FEE-TOTAL-AMT OF RSP06-OLD (OFEE-SUB).

           IF GFR1-FEE-TOTAL-AMT (GFEE-SUB) > ZERO  
              GO TO 3720-ADD-NEXT-FEE.  

           PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM GFEE-SUB BY 1   
                   UNTIL SUB > GFR1-FEE-ENTRY-CNT - 1   
              MOVE GFR1-FEE-TABLE (SUB + 1) TO  
                   GFR1-FEE-TABLE (SUB) 
           END-PERFORM. 

           SUBTRACT 1 FROM GFR1-FEE-ENTRY-CNT   
                    GIVING N18-SIGNED.  

           MOVE N18-UNSIGNED TO GFR1-FEE-ENTRY-CNT. 

       3760-CHECK-OLD-FEES. 
           IF WS-NEW-BKG-FLAG = 'Y' 
              GO TO 3800-UPDATE-GRP-FEE.

           MOVE ZERO TO OFEE-SUB.   

       3780-SUBTRACT-NEXT-FEE.  
           ADD 1 TO OFEE-SUB.   

           IF OFEE-SUB > RSP06-IFR-ENTRY-CTR OF RSP06-OLD   
              GO TO 3800-UPDATE-GRP-FEE.

           MOVE ZERO TO FEE-SUB.

           PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
                   UNTIL SUB > RSP06-IFR-ENTRY-CTR OF RSP06-NEW 
              IF RSP06-IFR-FEE-CODE OF RSP06-OLD (OFEE-SUB) =   
                    RSP06-IFR-FEE-CODE OF RSP06-NEW (SUB)   
                 MOVE SUB TO FEE-SUB
                 MOVE RSP06-IFR-ENTRY-CTR OF RSP06-NEW TO SUB   
              END-IF
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF FEE-SUB > ZERO
              GO TO 3780-SUBTRACT-NEXT-FEE. 

           MOVE ZERO TO GFEE-SUB.   

           PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
                   UNTIL SUB > GFR1-FEE-ENTRY-CNT   
              IF RSP06-IFR-FEE-CODE OF RSP06-OLD (OFEE-SUB) =   
                    GFR1-FEE-CODE (SUB) 
                 MOVE SUB                TO GFEE-SUB
                 MOVE GFR1-FEE-ENTRY-CNT TO SUB 
              END-IF
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF GFEE-SUB = ZERO   
              GO TO 3780-SUBTRACT-NEXT-FEE. 

           SUBTRACT RSP06-IFR-FEE-TOTAL-AMT OF RSP06-OLD (OFEE-SUB) FROM
                    GFR1-FEE-TOTAL-AMT (GFEE-SUB).  

           MOVE 'Y' TO WS-UPDATE-IND.   

           IF GFR1-FEE-TOTAL-AMT (GFEE-SUB) > ZERO  
              GO TO 3780-SUBTRACT-NEXT-FEE. 

           PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM GFEE-SUB BY 1   
                   UNTIL SUB > GFR1-FEE-ENTRY-CNT - 1   
              MOVE GFR1-FEE-TABLE (SUB + 1) TO  
                   GFR1-FEE-TABLE (SUB) 
           END-PERFORM. 

           SUBTRACT 1 FROM GFR1-FEE-ENTRY-CNT   
                    GIVING N18-SIGNED.  

           MOVE N18-UNSIGNED TO GFR1-FEE-ENTRY-CNT. 

       3800-UPDATE-GRP-FEE. 
           IF WS-UPDATE-IND NOT = 'Y'   
              GO TO 3820-UPDATE-GROUP-CABIN-COUNTS. 

           IF GFR1-FEE-ENTRY-CNT = ZERO 
              IF WS-STORE-IND = 'Y' 
                 GO TO 3820-UPDATE-GROUP-CABIN-COUNTS   
              ELSE  
                 DELETE GRP1-FEE-REC RECORD 
                        ON ERROR GO TO 28700-DELETE-GFR1-ERROR  
                 GO TO 3820-UPDATE-GROUP-CABIN-COUNTS.  

           IF WS-STORE-IND = 'Y'
              STORE GRP1-FEE-REC
                     ON ERROR GO TO 28800-STORE-GFR1-ERROR  
              GO TO 3820-UPDATE-GROUP-CABIN-COUNTS. 

           MODIFY GRP1-FEE-REC RECORD   
                  ON ERROR GO TO 24000-MODIFY-GFR1-ERROR.

              IF RSP06-IBR-CANCEL-DATE OF RSP06-OLD > ZERO  
                 IF RSP06-IBR-CANCEL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW > ZERO   
                    GO TO 3840-ESTABLISH-OLD-CNTS   
                 END-IF 
              ELSE  
                 GO TO 3840-ESTABLISH-OLD-CNTS. 

       COPY RSP37INT REPLACING RSP37-GROUP-COUNT-INFO BY RSP37-OLD. 

           GO TO 3860-SET-NEW-CNTS. 

       3840-ESTABLISH-OLD-CNTS.

108267     COMPUTE WS-GBR1-CAL-COURTESY-AMT ROUNDED =   
108267                                        GBR1-COURTESY-ORDER-AMT *

207936         GO TO 4100-CHECK-SSCIR

PPC470*8050-LOAD-DEV-TABLE. 
PPC470*    MOVE ZERO TO DEV-SUB.

PPC470*    PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL SUB > DEV-CNT  
PPC470*       IF RSP06-PAX-DEV-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) =
PPC470*             WS-DEV-SEQ-NUM (SUB)
PPC470*          MOVE SUB     TO DEV-SUB
PPC470*          MOVE DEV-CNT TO SUB
PPC470*       END-IF
PPC470*    END-PERFORM. 

PPC470*    IF DEV-SUB > ZERO
PPC470*       GO TO 8052-LOAD-PAX-DEV-TABLE.

PPC470*    ADD 1 TO DEV-CNT.

PPC470*    IF DEV-CNT > DEV-MAX 
PPC470*       MOVE 229 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
PPC470*       GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

PPC470*    MOVE DEV-CNT TO DEV-SUB. 

PPC470*    IF RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE OF RSP06-OLD = 'G'
PPC470*       MOVE LOW-VALUES TO WS-DEV-ENTRY (DEV-SUB) 
PPC470*       MOVE RSP06-PAX-DEV-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) TO 
PPC470*            WS-DEV-SEQ-NUM (DEV-SUB) 
PPC470*       GO TO 8052-LOAD-PAX-DEV-TABLE.

PPC470*    MOVE RSP06-PAX-DEV-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) TO
PPC470*         IDCR-SEQ-NUM.   

PPC470*    FETCH INDV-DEV-CITY-REC VIA INDV-BOOKING-DEVIATION-SET   
PPC470*          USING IDCR-SEQ-NUM 
PPC470*          ON ERROR GO TO 27500-FETCH-IDCR-ERROR. 

PPC470*    MOVE IDCR-SEQ-NUM           TO WS-DEV-SEQ-NUM (DEV-SUB). 
PPC470*    MOVE IDCR-SEATS-BLK         TO WS-DEV-BLK-CNT (DEV-SUB). 
PPC470*    MOVE IDCR-SEATS-BTH         TO WS-DEV-BTH-CNT (DEV-SUB). 
PPC470*    MOVE IDCR-SEATS-CAX         TO WS-DEV-CAX-CNT (DEV-SUB). 
PPC470*    MOVE IDCR-SEATS-REL         TO WS-DEV-REL-CNT (DEV-SUB). 
PPC470*    MOVE IDCR-SEATS-MOD         TO WS-DEV-MOD-CNT (DEV-SUB). 
PPC470*    MOVE IDCR-DEV-ADMIN-FEE-AMT TO   
PPC470*         WS-DEV-ADMIN-FEE-AMT (DEV-SUB). 

PPC470*    FIND CURRENT PAX-RECORD RECORD.  

PPC470*8052-LOAD-PAX-DEV-TABLE. 
PPC470*    IF RSP06-PAX-DEV-STATUS-IND OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) = 'A' OR 'M' 
PPC470*       NEXT SENTENCE 
PPC470*    ELSE 
PPC470*       GO TO 8059-EXIT.  

PPC470*    IF WS-DEV-MOD-CODE = 'M' 
PPC470*       IF RSP06-PAX-DEV-STATUS-IND OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) = 'M' 
PPC470*          GO TO 8059-EXIT
PPC470*       ELSE  
PPC470*          ADD 1 TO WS-DEV-MOD-CNT (DEV-SUB)  
PPC470*    ELSE 
PPC470*       ADD 1    TO WS-DEV-CAX-CNT (DEV-SUB). 

PPC470*    IF WS-DEV-BLK-CNT (DEV-SUB) > ZERO   
PPC470*       SUBTRACT 1 FROM WS-DEV-BLK-CNT (DEV-SUB)  
PPC470*                GIVING N18-SIGNED
PPC470*       MOVE N18-UNSIGNED TO WS-DEV-BLK-CNT (DEV-SUB) 
PPC470*    ELSE 
PPC470*       MOVE ZERO TO WS-DEV-BLK-CNT (DEV-SUB).

PPC470*    IF WS-DEV-BTH-CNT (DEV-SUB) > ZERO   
PPC470*       SUBTRACT 1 FROM WS-DEV-BTH-CNT (DEV-SUB)  
PPC470*                GIVING N18-SIGNED
PPC470*       MOVE N18-UNSIGNED TO WS-DEV-BTH-CNT (DEV-SUB) 
PPC470*    ELSE 
PPC470*       MOVE ZERO TO WS-DEV-BTH-CNT (DEV-SUB).

PPC470*    IF WS-DEV-MOD-CODE = 'M' 
PPC470*       MOVE 'M' TO RSP06-PAX-DEV-STATUS-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB)  
PPC470*       GO TO 8059-EXIT.  

PPC470*    IF RSP06-PAX-DEV-STATUS-IND OF RSP06-OLD (OSUB) = 'M'
PPC470*       IF WS-DEV-MOD-CNT (DEV-SUB) > ZERO
PPC470*          SUBTRACT 1 FROM WS-DEV-MOD-CNT (DEV-SUB)   
PPC470*                   GIVING N18-SIGNED 
PPC470*          MOVE N18-UNSIGNED TO WS-DEV-BLK-CNT (DEV-SUB)  
PPC470*       ELSE  
PPC470*          MOVE ZERO TO WS-DEV-MOD-CNT (DEV-SUB). 

PPC470*    IF NSUB > ZERO   
PPC470*       MOVE ZERO TO RSP06-PAX-DEV-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB)
PPC470*                    RSP06-PAX-DEV-ADDON-AMT OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB)  
PPC470*       MOVE SPACES TO
PPC470*            RSP06-PAX-DEV-STATUS-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NCSUB)
PPC470*    ELSE 
PPC470*       MOVE SPACES TO
PPC470*            RSP06-PCX-DEV-STATUS-IND OF RSP06-NEW (NCSUB)
PPC470*       MOVE ZERO TO  
PPC470*            RSP06-PCX-DEV-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (NCSUB)   
PPC470*            RSP06-PCX-DEV-ADDON-AMT OF RSP06-NEW (NCSUB).

       8100-UPDATE-NEW-DINING-CNTS.

           IF WS-DIN-GRP-IND = 'Y'  
              GO TO 8120-CHECK-WAITLIST.

           IF WS-OLD-DIN-PAX-CNT > ZERO 
              IF WS-NEW-DIN-PAX-CNT > ZERO  
                 IF WS-OLD-DIN-CNF = WS-NEW-DIN-CNF 
                    IF WS-OLD-DIN-PAX-CNT = WS-NEW-DIN-PAX-CNT  
                       GO TO 8120-CHECK-WAITLIST
                    END-IF  
                 END-IF 
              END-IF
           ELSE 
              IF WS-NEW-DIN-PAX-CNT = ZERO

       8110-NEXT-DIN-ENTRY. 
           ADD 1 TO DIN-SUB.

           IF DIN-SUB > 6   
              MOVE 230 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

PPC470*    IF SAIL1-DINING-TIME-CODE (DIN-SUB) <= SPACES
PPC470     IF TDP01-DINING-TIME-CODE (DIN-SUB) <= SPACES
              GO TO 8110-NEXT-DIN-ENTRY.

PPC470*       IF WS-NEW-DIN-CNF NOT = SAIL1-DINING-TIME-CODE (DIN-SUB)  
PPC470     IF (WS-OLD-DIN-CNF NOT = TDP01-DINING-TIME-CODE (DIN-SUB))   
PPC470        IF WS-NEW-DIN-CNF NOT = TDP01-DINING-TIME-CODE (DIN-SUB)  
                 GO TO 8110-NEXT-DIN-ENTRY  
              ELSE  
                 ADD WS-NEW-DIN-PAX-CNT TO  
PPC470*              SAIL1-GRP-DINING-CNT (DIN-SUB) 
PPC470               TDP01-GUESTS-ASSIGNED-CNT (DIN-SUB)
                 MOVE 'Y'    TO WS-UPDATE-IND   
                 MOVE ZERO   TO WS-NEW-DIN-PAX-CNT  
                 MOVE SPACES TO WS-NEW-DIN-CNF  
                 IF WS-OLD-DIN-PAX-CNT = ZERO   
                    GO TO 8120-CHECK-WAITLIST   
                 ELSE   
                    GO TO 8110-NEXT-DIN-ENTRY.  

PPC470*    IF WS-NEW-DIN-CNF NOT = SAIL1-DINING-TIME-CODE (DIN-SUB) 
PPC470*       IF WS-OLD-DIN-PAX-CNT > SAIL1-GRP-DINING-CNT (DIN-SUB)
PPC470*          MOVE ZERO   TO SAIL1-GRP-DINING-CNT (DIN-SUB)  
PPC470     IF WS-NEW-DIN-CNF NOT = TDP01-DINING-TIME-CODE (DIN-SUB) 
PPC470       IF WS-OLD-DIN-PAX-CNT > TDP01-GUESTS-ASSIGNED-CNT(DIN-SUB) 
PPC470           MOVE ZERO   TO TDP01-GUESTS-ASSIGNED-CNT (DIN-SUB) 
              ELSE  
                 SUBTRACT WS-OLD-DIN-PAX-CNT FROM   
PPC470*                   SAIL1-GRP-DINING-CNT (DIN-SUB)
PPC470                    TDP01-GUESTS-ASSIGNED-CNT (DIN-SUB)   
                          GIVING N18-SIGNED 
PPC470*          MOVE N18-UNSIGNED TO SAIL1-GRP-DINING-CNT (DIN-SUB)
PPC470           MOVE N18-UNSIGNED TO   
PPC470                            TDP01-GUESTS-ASSIGNED-CNT (DIN-SUB)   
              END-IF
              MOVE 'Y'    TO WS-UPDATE-IND  
              MOVE SPACES TO WS-OLD-DIN-CNF 
              MOVE ZERO   TO WS-OLD-DIN-PAX-CNT 
              IF WS-NEW-DIN-PAX-CNT = ZERO  
                 GO TO 8120-CHECK-WAITLIST  
              ELSE  
                 GO TO 8110-NEXT-DIN-ENTRY. 

           COMPUTE WS-DIN-ADJ-CNT = WS-NEW-DIN-PAX-CNT  
                                  - WS-OLD-DIN-PAX-CNT. 

           IF WS-NEW-DIN-PAX-CNT >= WS-OLD-DIN-PAX-CNT  
PPC470*       ADD WS-DIN-ADJ-CNT TO SAIL1-GRP-DINING-CNT (DIN-SUB)  
PPC470        ADD WS-DIN-ADJ-CNT TO TDP01-GUESTS-ASSIGNED-CNT (DIN-SUB) 
                  GIVING N18-SIGNED

PPC470        MOVE N18-UNSIGNED TO TDP01-GUESTS-ASSIGNED-CNT (DIN-SUB)  
              MOVE 'Y'    TO WS-UPDATE-IND  
              MOVE SPACES TO WS-NEW-DIN-CNF 
                             WS-OLD-DIN-CNF 
              MOVE ZERO   TO WS-NEW-DIN-PAX-CNT 
                             WS-OLD-DIN-PAX-CNT 
              GO TO 8120-CHECK-WAITLIST.

PPC470*    ADD WS-NEW-DIN-PAX-CNT TO SAIL1-GRP-DINING-CNT (DIN-SUB) 
PPC470     ADD WS-NEW-DIN-PAX-CNT TO TDP01-GUESTS-ASSIGNED-CNT (DIN-SUB)
               GIVING N18-SIGNED.

PPC470     MOVE N18-UNSIGNED TO TDP01-GUESTS-ASSIGNED-CNT (DIN-SUB).

PPC470*    IF WS-OLD-DIN-PAX-CNT > SAIL1-GRP-DINING-CNT (DIN-SUB)   
PPC470     IF WS-OLD-DIN-PAX-CNT > TDP01-GUESTS-ASSIGNED-CNT (DIN-SUB)  
              MOVE 231 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

           SUBTRACT WS-OLD-DIN-PAX-CNT FROM 
PPC470*             SAIL1-GRP-DINING-CNT (DIN-SUB)  
PPC470              TDP01-GUESTS-ASSIGNED-CNT (DIN-SUB) 
                    GIVING N18-SIGNED.

PPC470     MOVE N18-UNSIGNED TO TDP01-GUESTS-ASSIGNED-CNT (DIN-SUB).
           MOVE ZERO         TO WS-NEW-DIN-PAX-CNT  
                                WS-OLD-DIN-PAX-CNT. 
           MOVE 'Y'          TO WS-UPDATE-IND   
           MOVE SPACES       TO WS-NEW-DIN-CNF  
                                WS-OLD-DIN-CNF. 

       8120-CHECK-WAITLIST. 
           IF WS-NEW-BKG-FLAG = 'Y' 
              GO TO 8160-CHECK-NEW-WAITLIST.

           IF WS-OLD-DIN-PREF <= SPACES OR WS-OLD-DIN-PREF = 'U'

                 USING DWR-BOOK-NUM

              GO 8150-DELETE-DINING-WAITLIST.   

           IF WS-NEW-DIN-PREF <= SPACES OR WS-NEW-DIN-PREF = 'U'
              GO TO 8150-DELETE-DINING-WAITLIST.

           IF (RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW NOT =   
                 RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-OLD) OR   
                 (RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE OF RSP06-NEW NOT =
                 RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE OF RSP06-OLD) OR   
                 (RSP06-IBR-DURATION-DAYS OF RSP06-NEW NOT =
                 RSP06-IBR-DURATION-DAYS OF RSP06-OLD)  
              GO TO 8150-DELETE-DINING-WAITLIST.

           IF RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW <= DPP02-SYSTEM-DATE 
              GO TO 8150-DELETE-DINING-WAITLIST.

PPC470*                     SAIL1-GRP-DINING-WAIT-CNT (DIN-SUB) 
PPC470                      TDP01-GUESTS-WAIT-CNT (DIN-SUB) 
204833                      GIVING N18-SIGNED   
204833             MOVE N18-UNSIGNED      TO
PPC470*                            SAIL1-GRP-DINING-WAIT-CNT (DIN-SUB)  
PPC470                             TDP01-GUESTS-WAIT-CNT (DIN-SUB)  
204833             MOVE 'Y'               TO WS-UPDATE-IND  
204833             MOVE SPACES            TO WS-OLD-DIN-PREF
204833                                       WS-NEW-DIN-PREF
204833             MOVE ZERO              TO WS-NEW-DIN-PREF-CNT
204833                                       WS-OLD-DIN-PREF-CNT
204833          END-IF  
204833       END-IF 
204833  
204833     END-PERFORM. 

           IF DWR-DINING-CODE = WS-NEW-DIN-PREF 
              GO TO 8199-EXIT.  

           MOVE WS-NEW-DIN-PREF TO DWR-DINING-CODE. 

           MODIFY DINING-WAITLIST-REC RECORD
                  ON ERROR GO TO 29900-MODIFY-DWR-ERROR.

           GO TO 8199-EXIT. 

       8130-CHECK-SDR-ERROR.
           IF ERROR-NUM = '0013'
              GO TO 8160-CHECK-NEW-WAITLIST.

           MOVE 232 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX.

           GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR.   

       8140-CHECK-DWR-ERROR.
           IF ERROR-NUM = '0007' OR '0013'  
              GO TO 8160-CHECK-NEW-WAITLIST.

           MOVE 233 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX.

           GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR.   

       8150-DELETE-DINING-WAITLIST. 
204833     PERFORM VARYING DIN-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL DIN-SUB > 6
PPC470*      IF DWR-DINING-CODE = SAIL1-DINING-TIME-CODE (DIN-SUB)  
PPC470       IF DWR-DINING-CODE = TDP01-DINING-TIME-CODE (DIN-SUB)  
204833          AND WS-OLD-DIN-PREF-CNT > ZEROES
204833          IF WS-OLD-DIN-PREF-CNT >
PPC470*                            SAIL1-GRP-DINING-WAIT-CNT (DIN-SUB)  
PPC470                             TDP01-GUESTS-WAIT-CNT (DIN-SUB)  
204833             MOVE ZERO              TO
PPC470*                            SAIL1-GRP-DINING-WAIT-CNT (DIN-SUB)  
PPC470                             TDP01-GUESTS-WAIT-CNT (DIN-SUB)  
204833          ELSE
204833             SUBTRACT WS-OLD-DIN-PREF-CNT FROM
PPC470*                     SAIL1-GRP-DINING-WAIT-CNT (DIN-SUB) 
PPC470                      TDP01-GUESTS-WAIT-CNT (DIN-SUB) 
204833                      GIVING N18-SIGNED   
204833             MOVE N18-UNSIGNED      TO
PPC470*                            SAIL1-GRP-DINING-WAIT-CNT (DIN-SUB)  
PPC470                             TDP01-GUESTS-WAIT-CNT (DIN-SUB)  
204833          END-IF  
204833          MOVE ZEROES               TO WS-OLD-DIN-PREF-CNT
204833          MOVE 'Y'                  TO WS-UPDATE-IND  
204833        END-IF
204833     END-PERFORM. 
           DELETE DINING-WAITLIST-REC RECORD
                  ON ERROR GO TO 27700-DELETE-DWR-ERROR.

       8160-CHECK-NEW-WAITLIST. 
           IF WS-DIN-GRP-IND = 'Y'  
              GO 8199-EXIT. 

           IF WS-NEW-DIN-PREF <= SPACES OR WS-NEW-DIN-PREF = 'U'
              GO TO 8199-EXIT.  

           IF RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW <= DPP02-SYSTEM-DATE 
              GO TO 8199-EXIT.  

           MOVE ZERO TO DIN-SUB.

           PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL SUB > 6
PPC470*       IF WS-NEW-DIN-PREF = SAIL1-DINING-TIME-CODE (SUB) 
PPC470        IF WS-NEW-DIN-PREF = TDP01-DINING-TIME-CODE (SUB) 
                 MOVE SUB TO DIN-SUB
                 MOVE 6   TO SUB
              END-IF
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF DIN-SUB = ZERO
              MOVE 234 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

PPC470*    IF SAIL1-GRP-DINING-MAX (DIN-SUB) <= ZERO
PPC470     IF TDP01-MAX-GUESTS (DIN-SUB) <= ZERO
              GO TO 8199-EXIT.  

PPC470*    IF (SAIL1-DINING-CAP-MET-IND (DIN-SUB) NOT = 'Y')
PPC470*       FIND CURRENT SAIL1-REC RECORD 
PPC470*            ON ERROR GO TO 29700-FIND-SAIL1-ERROR
PPC470*       MOVE 'Y' TO SAIL1-DINING-CAP-MET-IND (DIN-SUB)
PPC470*       MODIFY SAIL1-REC RECORD   
PPC470*       ON ERROR GO TO 29800-MODIFY-SAIL1-ERROR.  

PPC470     IF (TDP01-CAPACITY-MET-IND (DIN-SUB) NOT = 'Y')  
PPC470        MOVE 'Y' TO TDP01-CAPACITY-MET-IND (DIN-SUB)  
PPC470        EXEC SQL  
PPC470             UPDATE SAIL_DINING   
PPC470             SET CAPACITY_MET_IND  =  
PPC470                              :TDP01-CAPACITY-MET-IND(DIN-SUB)
PPC470              WHERE SHIP_CODE = :TDP01-DIN-SHIP-CODE  
PPC470                AND SAIL_ID   = :TDP01-DIN-SAIL-ID
PPC470                AND DINING_TIME_CODE =

PPC470        END-EXEC  
PPC470        IF SQLCODE < 0 OR SQLCODE = 100   
PPC470           GO TO 29801-MODIFY-SAIL1-ERROR 
PPC470        END-IF
PPC470     END-IF.  

           MOVE RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW TO SDR-SAIL-DATE.  
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE OF RSP06-NEW TO SDR-SHIP-CODE.  
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-DURATION-DAYS OF RSP06-NEW TO 
                SDR-DURATION-DAYS.  
           MOVE 2 TO FETCH-WITH-LOCK.   

           FETCH SAIL-DINING-REC RECORD 
                 ON ERROR GO TO 8170-CHECK-SDR-ERROR.   

           MOVE ZERO TO FETCH-WITH-LOCK.

           GO TO 8190-STORE-DWR-REC.

       8170-CHECK-SDR-ERROR.
           IF ERROR-NUM NOT = '0013'
              MOVE 0 TO FETCH-WITH-LOCK 
              MOVE 235 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

           FETCH SAIL-DINING-DIRECT-REC RECORD  
                 ON ERROR GO TO 8180-CHECK-SDDR-ERROR.  

           MOVE ZERO TO FETCH-WITH-LOCK.
           MOVE LOW-VALUES TO SAIL-DINING-REC.  
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW TO SDR-SAIL-DATE.  
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE OF RSP06-NEW TO SDR-SHIP-CODE.  
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-DURATION-DAYS OF RSP06-NEW TO 
                SDR-DURATION-DAYS.  

           STORE SAIL-DINING-REC
                 ON ERROR GO TO 27800-STORE-SDR-ERROR.  

           GO TO 8190-STORE-DWR-REC.

       8180-CHECK-SDDR-ERROR.   
           IF ERROR-NUM NOT = '0013'
              MOVE 0 TO FETCH-WITH-LOCK 
              MOVE 236 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

           MOVE ZERO       TO FETCH-WITH-LOCK.  
           MOVE LOW-VALUES TO SAIL-DINING-DIRECT-REC
                              SAIL-DINING-REC.  
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW TO SDR-SAIL-DATE.  
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE OF RSP06-NEW TO SDR-SHIP-CODE.  
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-DURATION-DAYS OF RSP06-NEW TO 
                SDR-DURATION-DAYS.  

           STORE SAIL-DINING-DIRECT-REC 
                 ON ERROR GO TO 27900-STORE-SDDR-ERROR. 

           STORE SAIL-DINING-REC
                 ON ERROR GO TO 28000-STORE-SDR-ERROR.  

       8190-STORE-DWR-REC.  
           MOVE LOW-VALUES      TO DINING-WAITLIST-REC. 
           MOVE WS-NEW-DIN-PREF                  TO DWR-DINING-CODE.
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-DATE OF RSP06-NEW TO DWR-BOOK-DATE.  
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TIME OF RSP06-NEW TO DWR-BOOK-TIME.  
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM  OF RSP06-NEW TO DWR-BOOK-NUM.   

           STORE DINING-WAITLIST-REC
                 ON ERROR GO TO 28100-STORE-DWR-ERROR.  

204833     PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL SUB > 6
PPC470*      IF DWR-DINING-CODE = SAIL1-DINING-TIME-CODE (DIN-SUB)

              GO TO 8810-REMOVE-ALTERNATE.  

           IF RECORD MEMBER OF AGENT-ACTIVE-BKGS-SET SET
              REMOVE AGENT-COMP-BOOKINGS-REC RECORD 
                FROM AGENT-ACTIVE-BKGS-SET  
                ON ERROR GO TO 21200-REMOVE-ACTIVE-ERROR
           ELSE 
              IF RECORD MEMBER OF AGENT-CXF-BKGS-SET SET
                 REMOVE AGENT-COMP-BOOKINGS-REC RECORD  
                   FROM AGENT-CXF-BKGS-SET  
                   ON ERROR GO TO 21300-REMOVE-CXF-BKG-ERROR.

           IF RECORD MEMBER OF AGENT-ALT-ACTIVE-BKGS-SET SET
              REMOVE AGENT-COMP-BOOKINGS-REC RECORD 
                FROM AGENT-ALT-ACTIVE-BKGS-SET  
                ON ERROR GO TO 21400-REMOVE-ALT-ACT-ERROR

              IF RECORD MEMBER OF AGENT-ALT-CXF-BKGS-SET SET
                 REMOVE AGENT-COMP-BOOKINGS-REC RECORD  
                   FROM AGENT-ALT-CXF-BKGS-SET  
                   ON ERROR GO TO 21500-REMOVE-ALT-CXF-ERROR.   

       8820-INSERT-MAIN.
           IF WS-NEW-BKG-FLAG = 'N' 
139587        IF WS-NEW-MAIN-FLAG = 'N' AND WS-NEW-STATUS-FLAG = 'N' AND
139587           WS-SAIL-CHG-FLAG = 'N' 
                 GO TO 8850-INSERT-ALTERNATE.   

           IF IBR-CUSTOMER-NUM NOT = AGENT-CUSTOMER-NUM 
              MOVE SPACES  TO AAR-CUSTOMER-KEY  
              MOVE 'CM'                   TO AAR-CUSTOMER-TYPE  
              MOVE RSP06-IBR-CUSTOMER-NUM OF RSP06-NEW TO   
                   AAR-CUSTOMER-NUM 
              FETCH AGENCY-ASSOCIATION-REC RECORD   
                    ON ERROR GO TO 21600-FETCH-AAR-ERROR
              FETCH OWNER WITHIN AGENCY-ASSOCIATION-SET SET 
                    ON ERROR GO TO 21700-FETCH-AGENT-ERROR. 

           IF AGENT-PHONE-NUM  = ATCR-PHONE-NUM AND 
                 IBR-COMPANY-CODE = ATCR-COMPANY-CODE   
              FIND CURRENT AGENT-TERMS-COMPANY-REC RECORD   
                   ON ERROR GO TO 21800-FIND-ATCR-ERROR 
           ELSE 
              MOVE AGENT-PHONE-NUM  TO ATCR-PHONE-NUM.  
              MOVE IBR-COMPANY-CODE TO ATCR-COMPANY-CODE.   
              FETCH AGENT-TERMS-COMPANY-REC RECORD  
                    ON ERROR GO TO 21900-FETCH-ATCR-ERROR.  

           PERFORM DPP20-UNLOAD-ATCR-DATES THRU DPP20-EXIT. 

           IF WS-NEW-BKG-FLAG = 'Y' 
              MOVE DPP02-SYSTEM-DATE TO DPP20-ATCR-DATE-LAST-BOOKING
              PERFORM DPP20-LOAD-ATCR-DATES THRU DPP20-EXIT 
              MODIFY AGENT-TERMS-COMPANY-REC RECORD 
                     ON ERROR GO TO 22000-MODIFY-ATCR-ERROR.

           MOVE DPP02-SAIL-MM      TO AMYR-MONTH
           MOVE DPP02-SAIL-YYYY    TO AMYR-YEAR 

           FETCH AGENT-MO-YR-REC VIA AGENT-MO-YR-SET
                 USING AMYR-CCYYMM  
114465           ON ERROR GO TO 8830-STORE-AMYR.

       8900-AUTO-UNALLOCATE.
           IF RSP06-IBR-BOOK-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW NOT = 'G'
              GO TO 8999-EXIT.  

           MOVE ZEROS TO WS-UNALLOC-UCC-AMT 
                         WS-UNALLOC-CASH-AMT
                         WS-UNALLOC-OTHER-AMT.  

           COMPUTE WS-UNALLOC-AMT = 
                                 RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-CASH-PMT OF RSP06-NEW  
                               + RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-UCC-PMT OF RSP06-NEW   
                               + RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-OTHER-PMT OF RSP06-NEW 
                               + RSP06-IBR-CABIN-CASH-PMT OF RSP06-NEW  
                               + RSP06-IBR-CABIN-UCC-PMT OF RSP06-NEW   
                               + RSP06-IBR-CABIN-OTHER-PMT OF RSP06-NEW 
                               - RSP06-IBR-NET-FARE-AMT OF RSP06-NEW
                               - RSP06-IBR-PROGRAM-FEE-AMT OF RSP06-NEW.

           IF WS-UNALLOC-AMT <= ZERO
              GO TO 8999-EXIT.  

           IF WS-UNALLOC-AMT > RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-UCC-PMT  OF RSP06-NEW
              SUBTRACT RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-UCC-PMT OF RSP06-NEW 
                  FROM WS-UNALLOC-AMT   
              MOVE RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-UCC-PMT OF RSP06-NEW TO  
                   WS-UNALLOC-UCC-AMT   
              MOVE ZERO TO RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-UCC-PMT OF RSP06-NEW 
           ElSE 
              SUBTRACT WS-UNALLOC-AMT   
                  FROM RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-UCC-PMT OF RSP06-NEW 
              MOVE WS-UNALLOC-AMT TO WS-UNALLOC-UCC-AMT 
              GO TO 8910-AUTO-UNALLOCATE-HISTORY.   

           IF WS-UNALLOC-AMT <= ZERO
              GO TO 8910-AUTO-UNALLOCATE-HISTORY.   

           IF WS-UNALLOC-AMT > RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-CASH-PMT OF RSP06-NEW
              SUBTRACT RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-CASH-PMT OF RSP06-NEW
                  FROM WS-UNALLOC-AMT   
              MOVE RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-CASH-PMT OF RSP06-NEW TO 
                   WS-UNALLOC-CASH-AMT  
              MOVE ZEROS TO RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-CASH-PMT OF RSP06-NEW   
           ElSE 
              SUBTRACT WS-UNALLOC-AMT   
                  FROM RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-CASH-PMT OF RSP06-NEW
              MOVE WS-UNALLOC-AMT TO WS-UNALLOC-CASH-AMT
              GO TO 8910-AUTO-UNALLOCATE-HISTORY.   

           IF WS-UNALLOC-AMT <= ZERO
              GO TO 8910-AUTO-UNALLOCATE-HISTORY.   

           IF WS-UNALLOC-AMT > RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-OTHER-PMT OF RSP06-NEW   
              SUBTRACT RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-OTHER-PMT OF RSP06-NEW   
                  FROM WS-UNALLOC-AMT   
              MOVE RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-OTHER-PMT OF RSP06-NEW TO
                   WS-UNALLOC-OTHER-AMT 
              MOVE ZEROS TO RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-OTHER-PMT OF RSP06-NEW  
           ElSE 
              SUBTRACT WS-UNALLOC-AMT   
                  FROM RSP06-IBR-ALLOC-OTHER-PMT OF RSP06-NEW   
              MOVE WS-UNALLOC-AMT TO WS-UNALLOC-OTHER-AMT.  

       8910-AUTO-UNALLOCATE-HISTORY.
           IF WS-UNALLOC-CASH-AMT <= ZERO   
              GO TO 8920-CHECK-UCC-CHANGE.  

           ADD 1 TO RSP29-HIST-CNT. 

           IF RSP29-HIST-CNT > RSP29-HIST-MAX   
              MOVE 243 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

            MOVE SPACES     TO RSP29-HIST-AN-PARAMS (RSP29-HIST-CNT).   
            MOVE LOW-VALUES TO RSP29-HIST-N-PARAMS (RSP29-HIST-CNT).
            MOVE 'UAL'      TO RSP29-HIST-ACTIVITY-TYPE (RSP29-HIST-CNT).   
            MOVE RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-NEW TO
                 RSP29-HIST-CABIN-NUM (RSP29-HIST-CNT)  
            MOVE RSP06-IBR-AGENT-CONTACT OF RSP06-NEW TO
                 RSP29-HIST-CONTACT (RSP29-HIST-CNT).   
            MOVE WS-UNALLOC-CASH-AMT TO 
                 RSP29-HIST-AMT (RSP29-HIST-CNT)
                 WS-DSPAMT-8-1. 

            STRING 'Group Cabin Auto-UnAllocated Cash: ' DELIMITED SIZE 
                   WS-DSPAMT-8-1  DELIMITED SPACE   
                   INTO RSP29-HIST-COMMENT (RSP29-HIST-CNT).

       8920-CHECK-UCC-CHANGE.   
           IF WS-UNALLOC-UCC-AMT <= ZERO
              GO TO 8930-CHECK-OTHER-CHANGE.

           ADD 1 TO RSP29-HIST-CNT. 

           IF RSP29-HIST-CNT > RSP29-HIST-MAX   
              MOVE 244 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

            MOVE SPACES     TO RSP29-HIST-AN-PARAMS (RSP29-HIST-CNT).   
            MOVE LOW-VALUES TO RSP29-HIST-N-PARAMS (RSP29-HIST-CNT).
            MOVE 'UAL'      TO RSP29-HIST-ACTIVITY-TYPE (RSP29-HIST-CNT).   
            MOVE RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-NEW TO
                 RSP29-HIST-CABIN-NUM (RSP29-HIST-CNT)  
            MOVE RSP06-IBR-AGENT-CONTACT OF RSP06-NEW TO
                 RSP29-HIST-CONTACT (RSP29-HIST-CNT).   
            MOVE WS-UNALLOC-UCC-AMT TO  
                 RSP29-HIST-AMT (RSP29-HIST-CNT)
                 WS-DSPAMT-8-1. 

            STRING 'Group Cabin Auto-UnAllocated CC: ' DELIMITED SIZE   
                   WS-DSPAMT-8-1 DELIMITED SPACE
                   INTO RSP29-HIST-COMMENT (RSP29-HIST-CNT).

       8930-CHECK-OTHER-CHANGE. 
           IF WS-UNALLOC-OTHER-AMT <= ZERO  
              GO TO 8999-EXIT.  

           ADD 1 TO RSP29-HIST-CNT. 

           IF RSP29-HIST-CNT > RSP29-HIST-MAX   
              MOVE 245 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
              GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG.

            MOVE SPACES     TO RSP29-HIST-AN-PARAMS (RSP29-HIST-CNT).   
            MOVE LOW-VALUES TO RSP29-HIST-N-PARAMS (RSP29-HIST-CNT).
            MOVE 'UAL'      TO RSP29-HIST-ACTIVITY-TYPE (RSP29-HIST-CNT).   
            MOVE RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM OF RSP06-NEW TO
                 RSP29-HIST-CABIN-NUM (RSP29-HIST-CNT)  
            MOVE RSP06-IBR-AGENT-CONTACT OF RSP06-NEW TO
                 RSP29-HIST-CONTACT (RSP29-HIST-CNT).   
            MOVE WS-UNALLOC-OTHER-AMT TO
                 RSP29-HIST-AMT (RSP29-HIST-CNT)
                 WS-DSPAMT-8-1. 

            STRING 'Group Cabin Auto-UnAllocated Other: ' DELIMITED SIZE
                   WS-DSPAMT-8-1 DELIMITED SPACE
                   INTO RSP29-HIST-COMMENT (RSP29-HIST-CNT).

       8999-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

194736 8950-FETCH-TOAR-REC. 
194736     EXEC SQL DECLARE BUILD_TARGET_OFFER CURSOR   
194736              SELECT  OFF.IDENTIFIER_TYPE,
194736                      OFF.IDENTIFIER, 
194736                      OFF.OFFER_ID,   
194736                      OFF.SYNC_STATUS_CODE,   
194736                      OFF.WITHDRAWN_IND,  
194736                      OFF.DECLINED_IND,   
194736                      OFF.REDEMPTION_CNT, 
194736                      MST.TYPE_CODE,  
194736                      MST.STATUS_CODE,
PPC470                      MST.ENTITY_CODE_1,  
PPC470*                     MST.PROMOTION_CODE, 
PPC470*                     CONVERT(MST.BOOK_BEGIN_DATE,112),   
PPC470*                     CONVERT(MST.BOOK_END_DATE,112), 
PPC470                      CONVERT(MST.BOOK_BEGIN_DATETIME,112),   
PPC470                      CONVERT(MST.BOOK_END_DATETIME,112), 
194736                      RED.BOOK_NUM,   
194736                      RED.PAX_SEQ_NUM,
171943                      RED.SHIP_CODE,  
171943                      RED.SAIL_DATE,  
171943                      RED.DURATION_DAYS   
194736                FROM  TARGET_OFFER OFF
194736                JOIN  TARGET_OFFER_MASTER MST 
194736                ON    OFF.OFFER_ID = MST.OFFER_ID 
194736                LEFT  OUTER JOIN TARGET_REDEMPTION RED
194736                ON    (OFF.IDENTIFIER_TYPE = RED.IDENTIFIER_TYPE) 
194736                AND   (OFF.IDENTIFIER      = RED.IDENTIFIER)  
194736                AND   (OFF.OFFER_ID        = RED.OFFER_ID)
194736                WHERE (OFF.IDENTIFIER_TYPE = :WS-PERSON-TYPE) AND 
194736                      (OFF.IDENTIFIER      = :WS-PERSON-ID) AND   
194736                      (OFF.OFFER_ID = :WS-OFFER-ID)   
194736     END-EXEC.

194736     EXEC SQL 
194736          OPEN BUILD_TARGET_OFFER 
194736     END-EXEC.

PPC470     IF SQLCODE < 0   
PPC470        GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
PPC470     END-IF.

PPC470     END-IF.

214641 D300-UPDATE-PBS-IND.

214641     END-IF.

214641          CHANGE SET (UPDATE_DATETIME   = :WS-UPDATE-DATETIME,
214641                      PBS_AVAILABLE_IND = :WS-PBS-AVAILABLE-IND   
214641                                         :WS-PBS-AVAILABLE-IND-NI)
214641                 WHERE BOOK_NUM         = :WS-IBR-BOOK-NUM    AND 
                            SHIP_CODE = :WS-IBR-SHIP-CODE AND   
214641                  (UPDATE_DATETIME <> :WS-UPDATE-DATETIME     OR  
214641                  ((:WS-PBS-AVAILABLE-IND-NI = -1             AND 
214641                  PBS_AVAILABLE_IND IS NOT NULL)              OR  
214641                  (:WS-PBS-AVAILABLE-IND-NI = 0               AND 
214641                  PBS_AVAILABLE_IND IS NULL)                  OR  
214641                  PBS_AVAILABLE_IND <> :WS-PBS-AVAILABLE-IND))
214641     END-EXEC.
PPC470     IF SQLCODE < 0   
              GO TO 10250-MODIFY-CBK-ERROR  
           END-IF.

PPC470     MOVE 'Y'    TO WS-GUEST-OFFER-INSERT-IND.
PPC470     GO TO 40400-CHECK-OFFER-COMPONENT.

PPC470 40300-UPDATE-GST-OFF-TBL.

                      OFFER_ID = :RSP06-OFFER-ID
                                  OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NOFF-SUB) AND
                      IFNULL(GUEST_SEQ_NUM,0) = :RSP06-GUEST-SEQ-NUM OF 
PPC470                                     RSP06-NEW (WS-NOFF-SUB) AND  
PPC470                OFFER_ID = :RSP06-OFFER-ID OF 
PPC470                                     RSP06-NEW (WS-NOFF-SUB)  
PPC470     END-EXEC.
PPC470  
PPC470     IF SQLCODE < 0 OR SQLCODE = 100  
PPC470        GO TO 35200-UPDATE-GUEST-OFFER-ERR
PPC470     END-IF.  
PPC470  
PPC470     MOVE 'N'    TO WS-GUEST-OFFER-INSERT-IND.
PPC470  
PPC470 40400-CHECK-OFFER-COMPONENT. 
PPC470  
PPC470     MOVE 0 TO WS-NCOMP-SUB.  
PPC470  
PPC470 40500-LOOP-OFFER-COMPONENT.  
PPC470     ADD 1 TO WS-NCOMP-SUB.   
PPC470  
PPC470     IF WS-NCOMP-SUB > RSP06-ENTITY-CNT OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NOFF-SUB)
PPC470        GO TO 41100-CHECK-OFF-COMP-DELETE 
PPC470     END-IF.  
PPC470  
PPC470     IF WS-NEW-BKG-FLAG = 'Y' 
PPC470        GO TO 40600-INSERT-OFFER-COMPONENT
PPC470     ELSE 
PPC470        IF RSP06-GST-OFFER-READ-IND OF RSP06-OLD NOT = 'Y'
PPC470           MOVE 818 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX   
PPC470           GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG  
PPC470        END-IF
PPC470        IF RSP06-GST-OFFER-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-OLD NOT = 'Y'   
PPC470           GO TO 40600-INSERT-OFFER-COMPONENT 
PPC470        ELSE  
PPC470           IF RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE OF RSP06-OLD NOT =  
PPC470              RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE OF RSP06-NEW
PPC470              GO TO 40600-INSERT-OFFER-COMPONENT  
PPC470           END-IF 
PPC470           PERFORM VARYING WS-OOFF-SUB FROM 1 BY 1
PPC470                   UNTIL WS-OOFF-SUB >
PPC470                              RSP06-GUEST-OFFER-CNT OF RSP06-OLD  
PPC470                  OR  
PPC470                   (RSP06-OFFER-ID OF RSP06-NEW  (WS-NOFF-SUB) =  
PPC470                       RSP06-OFFER-ID OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OOFF-SUB)  
PPC470                  AND 
PPC470                   RSP06-GUEST-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NOFF-SUB) 
PPC470                                                               =  
PPC470                   RSP06-GUEST-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OOFF-SUB))
PPC470              CONTINUE
PPC470           END-PERFORM
PPC470           IF WS-OOFF-SUB > RSP06-GUEST-OFFER-CNT OF RSP06-OLD
PPC470              GO TO 40600-INSERT-OFFER-COMPONENT  
PPC470           ELSE   
PPC470              PERFORM VARYING WS-OCOMP-SUB FROM 1 BY 1
PPC470                      UNTIL WS-OCOMP-SUB >
PPC470                                 RSP06-ENTITY-CNT OF RSP06-OLD
PPC470                                                     (WS-OOFF-SUB)
PPC470                     OR   
PPC470                      (RSP06-ENTITY-TYPE OF RSP06-NEW 
PPC470                                    (WS-NOFF-SUB WS-NCOMP-SUB) =  
PPC470                          RSP06-ENTITY-TYPE OF RSP06-OLD  
PPC470                                    (WS-OOFF-SUB WS-OCOMP-SUB)
PPC470                     AND  
PPC470                      RSP06-ENTITY-CODE OF RSP06-NEW  
PPC470                                    (WS-NOFF-SUB WS-NCOMP-SUB) =  
PPC470                      RSP06-ENTITY-CODE OF RSP06-OLD  
PPC470                                    (WS-OOFF-SUB WS-OCOMP-SUB))   
PPC470                 CONTINUE 
PPC470              END-PERFORM 
PPC470              IF WS-OCOMP-SUB > RSP06-ENTITY-CNT OF RSP06-OLD 
PPC470                                                     (WS-OOFF-SUB)
PPC470                 GO TO 40600-INSERT-OFFER-COMPONENT   
PPC470              ELSE
PPC470                 IF RSP06-OFFER-COMPONENT-TABLE OF RSP06-NEW  
PPC470                                (WS-NOFF-SUB WS-NCOMP-SUB) NOT =  
PPC470                    RSP06-OFFER-COMPONENT-TABLE OF RSP06-OLD  
PPC470                                (WS-OOFF-SUB WS-OCOMP-SUB)
PPC470                    GO TO 40700-UPDATE-OFFER-COMPONENT
PPC470                 ELSE 
PPC470                    GO TO 40500-LOOP-OFFER-COMPONENT  
PPC470                 END-IF   
PPC470              END-IF  
PPC470           END-IF 
PPC470        END-IF
PPC470     END-IF.

PPC470        GO TO 35900-UPDATE-GST-QUAL-ERR   
PPC470     END-IF.  

PPC470 41700-UPDATE-GST-OFF-QUAL-EXIT.  
           EXIT.

PPC470 41800-CHECK-GST-QUAL-DELETE. 

PPC470     IF WS-NEW-BKG-FLAG = 'Y' 
PPC470        GO TO 41400-UPD-GST-OFF-QUAL-EXIT 
PPC470     END-IF.  

PPC470     IF RSP06-GST-OFF-QUAL-READ-IND OF RSP06-OLD NOT = 'Y'
PPC470        GO TO 41400-UPD-GST-OFF-QUAL-EXIT 
PPC470     ELSE 
PPC470        IF RSP06-GST-OFF-QUAL-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-OLD NOT = 'Y'
PPC470           GO TO 41400-UPD-GST-OFF-QUAL-EXIT  
PPC470        END-IF
PPC470     END-IF.  

PPC470     IF RSP06-GST-OFF-QUAL-READ-IND OF RSP06-NEW NOT = 'Y'
PPC470        MOVE 822 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX  
PPC470        GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG 
PPC470     END-IF.  

PPC470     IF RSP06-GST-OFF-QUAL-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-OLD NOT = 'Y'   
PPC470        GO TO 41400-UPD-GST-OFF-QUAL-EXIT 
PPC470     END-IF.  

PPC470     MOVE 0 TO WS-OQUAL-SUB.  
PPC470  
PPC470 41900-LOOP-OLD-GST-QUAL. 
PPC470  
PPC470     ADD 1 TO WS-OQUAL-SUB.   
PPC470  
PPC470     IF WS-OQUAL-SUB > RSP06-GUEST-QUAL-CNT OF RSP06-OLD  
PPC470        GO TO 41400-UPD-GST-OFF-QUAL-EXIT 
PPC470     END-IF.  
PPC470  
PPC470     IF RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE OF RSP06-OLD NOT =
PPC470        RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE OF RSP06-NEW  
PPC470        PERFORM VARYING WS-OINFO-SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
PPC470                UNTIL WS-OINFO-SUB > RSP06-GUEST-QUAL-INFO-CNT
PPC470                                      OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OQUAL-SUB) 
PPC470           PERFORM 42000-GST-OFF-QUAL-DELETE  
PPC470              THRU 42000-GST-OFF-QUAL-DELETE-EXIT 
PPC470        END-PERFORM   
PPC470        GO TO 41900-LOOP-OLD-GST-QUAL 
PPC470     END-IF.  
PPC470  
PPC470     PERFORM VARYING WS-NQUAL-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 
PPC470           UNTIL WS-NQUAL-SUB >   
PPC470                      RSP06-GUEST-QUAL-CNT OF RSP06-NEW OR
PPC470               (RSP06-GUEST-QUAL-SEQ-NUM  
PPC470                       OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OQUAL-SUB) =  
PPC470                RSP06-GUEST-QUAL-SEQ-NUM  
PPC470                       OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NQUAL-SUB)     AND
PPC470                RSP06-GUEST-QUAL-OFFER-ID 
PPC470                       OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OQUAL-SUB) =  
PPC470                RSP06-GUEST-QUAL-OFFER-ID 
PPC470                       OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NQUAL-SUB))   

PPC470         CONTINUE 
PPC470     END-PERFORM  
PPC470  
PPC470     IF WS-NQUAL-SUB > RSP06-GUEST-QUAL-CNT OF RSP06-NEW  
PPC470        PERFORM VARYING WS-OINFO-SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
PPC470                UNTIL WS-OINFO-SUB > RSP06-GUEST-QUAL-INFO-CNT
PPC470                                      OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OQUAL-SUB) 
PPC470           PERFORM 42000-GST-OFF-QUAL-DELETE  
PPC470              THRU 42000-GST-OFF-QUAL-DELETE-EXIT 
PPC470        END-PERFORM   
PPC470        GO TO 41900-LOOP-OLD-GST-QUAL 
PPC470     END-IF.  

PPC470     PERFORM VARYING WS-OINFO-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 
PPC470          UNTIL WS-OINFO-SUB >
PPC470                     RSP06-GUEST-QUAL-INFO-CNT
PPC470                            OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OQUAL-SUB)   
PPC470        PERFORM VARYING WS-NINFO-SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
PPC470             UNTIL WS-NINFO-SUB > 
PPC470                        RSP06-GUEST-QUAL-INFO-CNT 
PPC470                               OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NQUAL-SUB) OR 
PPC470                   ((RSP06-QUALIFICATION-CODE 
PPC470                     OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NQUAL-SUB WS-NINFO-SUB) =   
PPC470                     RSP06-QUALIFICATION-CODE 
PPC470                     OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OQUAL-SUB WS-OINFO-SUB)) AND
PPC470                    (RSP06-QUAL-TIER-CODE 
PPC470                     OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NQUAL-SUB WS-NINFO-SUB) =   
PPC470                     RSP06-QUAL-TIER-CODE 
PPC470                     OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OQUAL-SUB WS-OINFO-SUB)) AND
PPC470                    (RSP06-QUAL-STATE-CODE
PPC470                     OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NQUAL-SUB WS-NINFO-SUB) =   
PPC470                     RSP06-QUAL-STATE-CODE
PPC470                     OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OQUAL-SUB WS-OINFO-SUB)) AND
PPC470                    (RSP06-QUAL-COUNTRY-CODE  
PPC470                     OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NQUAL-SUB WS-NINFO-SUB) =   
PPC470                     RSP06-QUAL-COUNTRY-CODE  
PPC470                     OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OQUAL-SUB WS-OINFO-SUB)))   
PPC470           CONTINUE   
PPC470        END-PERFORM   
PPC470        IF WS-NINFO-SUB > RSP06-GUEST-QUAL-INFO-CNT   
PPC470                                    OF RSP06-NEW (WS-NQUAL-SUB)   
PPC470           PERFORM 42000-GST-OFF-QUAL-DELETE  
PPC470              THRU 42000-GST-OFF-QUAL-DELETE-EXIT 
PPC470        END-IF
PPC470     END-PERFORM. 
PPC470  
PPC470     GO TO 41900-LOOP-OLD-GST-QUAL.   
PPC470  
PPC470 42000-GST-OFF-QUAL-DELETE.   
PPC470  
PPC470     EXEC SQL 
PPC470         DELETE FROM GUEST_OFFER_QUAL 
PPC470         WHERE BOOK_NUM               = : RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM  
PPC470                                                 OF RSP06-OLD AND 
PPC470               SHIP_CODE              = :RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE  
PPC470                                                 OF RSP06-OLD AND 
PPC470               OFFER_ID               = :RSP06-GUEST-QUAL-OFFER-ID
PPC470                                  OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OQUAL-SUB) AND 
ppc470               ((GUEST_SEQ_NUM IS NULL AND
PPC470                 0 = :RSP06-GUEST-QUAL-SEQ-NUM
PPC470                                  OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OQUAL-SUB)) OR 
PPC470                 GUEST_SEQ_NUM          = 
PPC470                      :RSP06-GUEST-QUAL-SEQ-NUM   
PPC470                                 OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OQUAL-SUB)) AND 
PPC470               QUALIFICATION_CODE     = :RSP06-QUALIFICATION-CODE 
PPC470                     OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OQUAL-SUB WS-OINFO-SUB) AND 
PPC470               TIER_CODE              = :RSP06-QUAL-TIER-CODE 
PPC470                     OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OQUAL-SUB WS-OINFO-SUB) AND 
PPC470               QUALIFICATION_CODE     = :RSP06-QUALIFICATION-CODE 
PPC470                     OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OQUAL-SUB WS-OINFO-SUB) AND 
PPC470               TIER_CODE              = :RSP06-QUAL-TIER-CODE 
PPC470                     OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OQUAL-SUB WS-OINFO-SUB) AND 
PPC470               STATE_CODE             = :RSP06-QUAL-STATE-CODE
PPC470                     OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OQUAL-SUB WS-OINFO-SUB) AND 
PPC470               COUNTRY_CODE           = :RSP06-QUAL-COUNTRY-CODE  
PPC470                     OF RSP06-OLD (WS-OQUAL-SUB WS-OINFO-SUB) 
PPC470     END-EXEC.
PPC470  
PPC470     IF SQLCODE < 0 OR SQLCODE = 100  
PPC470        GO TO 36000-DELETE-GST-QUAL-ERR   
PPC470     END-IF.

CCLHP2 50000-UPDATE-HYB.
CCLHP2     MOVE SPACES                 TO WS-ORDER-NUM. 
CCLHP2     IF (RSP06-IBR-CANCEL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW > ZERO) AND   
CCLHP2        (RSP06-IBR-CANCEL-DATE OF RSP06-OLD <= ZERO) AND  
CCLHP2        (RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW > DPP02-SYSTEM-DATE)
CCLHP2  
CCLHP2        PERFORM 51000-FIND-ORDER  
CCLHP2           THRU 51000-FIND-ORDER-EXIT 
CCLHP2        IF SQLCODE < 0
CCLHP2           GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR  
CCLHP2        END-IF
CCLHP2  
CCLHP2        IF SQLCODE = 0 AND WS-ORDER-NUM > SPACES  
CCLHP2           MOVE 'CXF'            TO WS-ACTIVITY-CODE  
CCLHP2           MOVE 'Cancelled due to Booking Cancellation'   
CCLHP2                                 TO WS-COMMENT
CCLHP2           PERFORM 51000-INSERT-ORDER-ACTIVITY
CCLHP2              THRU 51000-INSERT-ORD-ACTIVITY-EXIT 
CCLHP2           IF SQLCODE < 0 
CCLHP2              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR   
CCLHP2           END-IF 
CCLHP2        END-IF
CCLHP2     END-IF.  
CCLHP2  
CCLHP2     IF WS-SAIL-CHG-FLAG = 'Y'
CCLHP2        MOVE SPACES TO WS-ORDER-NUM   
CCLHP2        PERFORM 51000-FIND-ORDER  
CCLHP2           THRU 51000-FIND-ORDER-EXIT 
CCLHP2        IF SQLCODE < 0
CCLHP2         GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR
CCLHP2        END-IF
CCLHP2        IF SQLCODE = 0 AND WS-ORDER-NUM > SPACES  
CCLHP2           IF RSP28-SAIL-SWAP-FLAG = 1
CCLHP2              MOVE 'CDS' TO WS-ACTIVITY-CODE  
CCLHP2              MOVE 'Booking Swap Change'  
CCLHP2                                 TO WS-COMMENT
CCLHP2           ELSE   
CCLHP2              MOVE 'CDT'         TO WS-ACTIVITY-CODE  
CCLHP2              MOVE 'Booking Sailing Change'   
CCLHP2                                    TO WS-COMMENT 
CCLHP2           END-IF 
CCLHP2           MOVE RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE OF RSP06-NEW  
CCLHP2                                 TO WS-SHIP-CODE-NEW  
CCLHP2           MOVE RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW (1:4)
CCLHP2                                 TO WS-YEAR-NEW   
CCLHP2           MOVE RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW (5:2)
CCLHP2                                 TO WS-MONTH-NEW  
CCLHP2           MOVE RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW (7:2)
CCLHP2                                 TO WS-DATE-NEW   
CCLHP2           MOVE RSP06-IBR-DURATION-DAYS OF RSP06-NEW  
CCLHP2                                 TO WS-DUR-DAYS-NEW   
CCLHP2           EXEC SQL   
CCLHP2              INSERT INTO ORDER_ACTIVITY  
CCLHP2                  (ACTIVITY_CODE  
CCLHP2                  ,BOOKING_NUM
CCLHP2                  ,ORDER_NUM  
CCLHP2                  ,NEW_SHIP_CODE  
CCLHP2                  ,NEW_SAIL_DATE  
CCLHP2                  ,NEW_DURATION_DAYS  
CCLHP2                 ,PAX_SEQ_NUM_1   
CCLHP2                 ,PAX_SEQ_NUM_2   
CCLHP2                 ,PAX_SEQ_NUM_3   
CCLHP2                 ,PAX_SEQ_NUM_4   
CCLHP2                 ,PAX_SEQ_NUM_5   
CCLHP2                 ,CHARGEBACK_AMT  
CCLHP2                 ,UPDATE_REMARKS  
CCLHP2                 ,CREATE_DATETIME)
CCLHP2              VALUES (:WS-ACTIVITY-CODE   
CCLHP2                 ,:RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW
CCLHP2                 ,NULL
CCLHP2                 ,:WS-SHIP-CODE-NEW   
CCLHP2                 ,:WS-SAIL-DATE-NEW   
CCLHP2                 ,:WS-DUR-DAYS-NEW
CCLHP2                 ,NULL
CCLHP2                 ,NULL
CCLHP2                 ,NULL
CCLHP2                 ,NULL
CCLHP2                 ,NULL
CCLHP2                 ,NULL
CCLHP2                 ,:WS-COMMENT 
CCLHP2                 ,GETUTCDATE ())  
CCLHP2           END-EXEC   
CCLHP2           IF SQLCODE < 0 
CCLHP2              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR   
CCLHP2           END-IF 
CCLHP2           EXEC SQL SELECT IDENTITY_VAL_LOCAL() INTO :WS-RECORD-ID
CCLHP2                       FROM RDMS.RDMS_DUMMY WHERE KEY_COL = 1 
CCLHP2                FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY
CCLHP2           END-EXEC   
CCLHP2  
CCLHP2           IF SQLCODE < 0 
CCLHP2              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR   
CCLHP2           END-IF 
CCLHP2  
CCLHP2           EXEC SQL   
CCLHP2               DELETE ORDER_ACTIVITY  
CCLHP2               WHERE  RECORD_ID = :WS-RECORD-ID   
CCLHP2           END-EXEC   
CCLHP2  
CCLHP2           IF SQLCODE < 0 
CCLHP2              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR   
CCLHP2           END-IF 
CCLHP2        END-IF
CCLHP2     END-IF.  
CCLHP2  
CCLHP2     IF (RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-OLD > RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-NEW) 
CCLHP2        AND (RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE OF RSP06-NEW > DPP02-SYSTEM-DATE)
CCLHP2        MOVE SPACES TO WS-ORDER-NUM   
CCLHP2        EXEC SQL  
CCLHP2            SELECT BKD.ORDER_NUM  
CCLHP2            INTO :WS-ORDER-NUM
CCLHP2            FROM BOOKING_ORDER BKD
CCLHP2            JOIN ORDER_MASTER OMS 
CCLHP2                 ON BKD.ORDER_NUM = OMS.ORDER_NUM 
CCLHP2            WHERE (BKD.BOOK_NUM = 
CCLHP2                   :RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW)  
CCLHP2*               AND (OMS.OBC_AMT = 0) 
CCLHP2                AND (OMS.ORDER_STATUS <> 'C') 
CCLHP2            FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY
CCLHP2        END-EXEC  
CCLHP2        IF SQLCODE < 0
CCLHP2           GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR  
CCLHP2        END-IF
CCLHP2        IF SQLCODE = 0 AND WS-ORDER-NUM > SPACES  
CCLHP2           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-PAX-NOT-FND-CNT  
CCLHP2           PERFORM VARYING SUB3 FROM 1 BY 1   
CCLHP2              UNTIL SUB3 > RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-OLD 
CCLHP2              MOVE 'N' TO WS-PAX-FOUND
CCLHP2              PERFORM VARYING SUB2 FROM 1 BY 1
CCLHP2                 UNTIL SUB2 > RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-NEW  
CCLHP2                 IF RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (SUB3) 
CCLHP2                    = RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (SUB2)   
CCLHP2                    MOVE RSP06-PAX-CNT OF RSP06-NEW TO SUB2   
CCLHP2                    MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PAX-FOUND  
CCLHP2                 END-IF   
CCLHP2              END-PERFORM 
CCLHP2              IF WS-PAX-FOUND = 'N'   
CCLHP2                 ADD 1 TO WS-PAX-NOT-FND-CNT  
CCLHP2                 MOVE RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-OLD (SUB3)   
CCLHP2                         TO WS-PAX-SEQ-NUM (WS-PAX-NOT-FND-CNT)   
CCLHP2              END-IF  
CCLHP2           END-PERFORM
CCLHP2           IF WS-PAX-NOT-FND-CNT > 0  
CCLHP2              MOVE ZEROES TO WS-PAX-SEQ-NUM-1 
CCLHP2                             WS-PAX-SEQ-NUM-2 
CCLHP2                             WS-PAX-SEQ-NUM-3 
CCLHP2                             WS-PAX-SEQ-NUM-4 
CCLHP2                             WS-PAX-SEQ-NUM-5 
CCLHP2              PERFORM VARYING SUB2 FROM 1 BY 1
CCLHP2                 UNTIL SUB2 > WS-PAX-NOT-FND-CNT  
CCLHP2                 EVALUATE SUB2
CCLHP2                   WHEN 1 
CCLHP2                     MOVE WS-PAX-SEQ-NUM (SUB2)   
CCLHP2                                   TO WS-PAX-SEQ-NUM-1
CCLHP2                   WHEN 2 
CCLHP2                     MOVE WS-PAX-SEQ-NUM (SUB2)   
CCLHP2                                   TO WS-PAX-SEQ-NUM-2
CCLHP2                   WHEN 3 
CCLHP2                     MOVE WS-PAX-SEQ-NUM (SUB2)   
CCLHP2                                   TO WS-PAX-SEQ-NUM-3
CCLHP2                   WHEN 4 
CCLHP2                     MOVE WS-PAX-SEQ-NUM (SUB2)   
CCLHP2                                   TO WS-PAX-SEQ-NUM-4
CCLHP2                   WHEN 5 
CCLHP2                     MOVE WS-PAX-SEQ-NUM (SUB2)   
CCLHP2                                   TO WS-PAX-SEQ-NUM-5
CCLHP2                 END-EVALUATE 
CCLHP2              END-PERFORM 
CCLHP2           END-IF 
CCLHP2           MOVE 'CXP'            TO WS-ACTIVITY-CODE  
CCLHP2           MOVE 'PAX CANCELLATION DONE ON THE BOOKING '   
CCLHP2                                 TO WS-COMMENT
CCLHP2           EXEC SQL   
CCLHP2              INSERT INTO ORDER_ACTIVITY  
CCLHP2                  (ACTIVITY_CODE  
CCLHP2                  ,BOOKING_NUM
CCLHP2                  ,ORDER_NUM  
CCLHP2                  ,NEW_SHIP_CODE  
CCLHP2                  ,NEW_SAIL_DATE  
CCLHP2                  ,NEW_DURATION_DAYS  
CCLHP2                 ,PAX_SEQ_NUM_1   
CCLHP2                 ,PAX_SEQ_NUM_2   
CCLHP2                 ,PAX_SEQ_NUM_3   
CCLHP2                 ,PAX_SEQ_NUM_4   
CCLHP2                 ,PAX_SEQ_NUM_5   
CCLHP2                 ,CHARGEBACK_AMT  
CCLHP2                 ,UPDATE_REMARKS  
CCLHP2                 ,CREATE_DATETIME)
CCLHP2              VALUES (:WS-ACTIVITY-CODE   
CCLHP2                 ,:RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM OF RSP06-NEW
CCLHP2                 ,NULL
CCLHP2                 ,NULL
CCLHP2                 ,NULL
CCLHP2                 ,NULL
CCLHP2                 ,:WS-PAX-SEQ-NUM-1   
CCLHP2                 ,:WS-PAX-SEQ-NUM-2   
CCLHP2                 ,:WS-PAX-SEQ-NUM-3   
CCLHP2                 ,:WS-PAX-SEQ-NUM-4   
CCLHP2                 ,:WS-PAX-SEQ-NUM-5   
CCLHP2                 ,NULL
CCLHP2                 ,:WS-COMMENT 
CCLHP2                 ,GETUTCDATE ())  
CCLHP2           END-EXEC

CCLHP2                       FROM RDMS.RDMS_DUMMY WHERE KEY_COL = 1 
CCLHP2                FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY
CCLHP2           END-EXEC

PAXOBC                           OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB, POBC), 
PAXOBC             :RSP06-POBC-OBC-CODE 
PAXOBC                           OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB, POBC), 
PAXOBC             :RSP06-POBC-MESSAGE  
PAXOBC                           OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB, POBC), 
PAXOBC             :RSP06-POBC-REASON   
PAXOBC                           OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB, POBC), 
PAXOBC             :RSP06-POBC-CHARGE-AMT   
PAXOBC                           OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB, POBC), 
PAXOBC             :RSP06-POBC-SAILING-AMT  
PAXOBC                           OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB, POBC), 
PAXOBC             :RSP06-POBC-PCT  
PAXOBC                           OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB, POBC), 
PAXOBC             :RSP06-POBC-USERID   
PAXOBC                           OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB, POBC), 
PAXOBC             :RSP06-POBC-PAYMENT-TYPE 
PAXOBC                           OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB, POBC), 
PAXOBC             :RSP06-POBC-ENTITY-TYPE  
PAXOBC                           OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB, POBC), 
PAXOBC             :RSP06-POBC-ENTITY-CODE

PAXOBC                           OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB, POBC)) 
PAXOBC          WHERE SHIP_CODE     =   
PAXOBC                    :RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE OF RSP06-NEW

PAXOBC                    :RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM  OF RSP06-NEW 
PAXOBC            AND GUEST_SEQ_NUM =   
PAXOBC                    :RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB)
PAXOBC            AND OBC_SEQ_NUM   = :POBC

PAXOBC               ((SRC_CODE <> :RSP06-POBC-SRC-CODE 
PAXOBC                           OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB, POBC)) 
PAXOBC            OR  (OBC_CODE <> :RSP06-POBC-OBC-CODE 
PAXOBC                           OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB, POBC)) 
PAXOBC            OR  (MESSAGE <> :RSP06-POBC-MESSAGE   
PAXOBC                           OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB, POBC))

PAXOBC                           OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB, POBC))

PAXOBC                           OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB, POBC)) 
PAXOBC            OR  (SAILING_AMT <> :RSP06-POBC-SAILING-AMT   
PAXOBC                           OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB, POBC)) 
PAXOBC            OR  (PERCENT <> :RSP06-POBC-PCT   
PAXOBC                           OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB, POBC)) 
PAXOBC            OR  (USERID <> :RSP06-POBC-USERID 
PAXOBC                           OF RSP06-NEW (NSUB, POBC))
